### [1] [States dressing analysis in a transmon-transmon-bus system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11514)
*P. A. Gladilovich,R. A. Migdisov,A. V. Sabluk,P. S. Burtsev,N. N. Abramov,N. Y. Rudenko,V. I. Chichkov,N. A. Maleeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验和理论研究了超导量子处理器中transmon-transmon-bus系统的计算态修饰（dressing）效应，通过三种方法估算修饰程度，并强调精确估计和控制计算态修饰对优化超导平台门操作的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器的多比特门保真度可能因计算态与非计算态之间的修饰而受限，需要深入理解和准确估计这种修饰效应以优化门操作。

Method: 实验上构建了transmon-transmon-bus系统，在宽范围内可调谐计算态修饰；理论上采用三种方法估算修饰：完整三元素模型、有效模式方法和未扰动模式方法。

Result: 三种方法得到的修饰估计结果揭示了准确估算和控制计算态修饰的重要性，为优化超导平台上的量子门提供指导。

Conclusion: 准确估计和控制计算态修饰是优化超导量子处理器门操作的关键因素。

Abstract: The multi-qubit gates fidelity of superconducting quantum processors can be limited due to the dressing of computational states by noncomputational ones. Here, we experimentally and analytically investigate a transmon-transmon-bus system where the computational states dressing is tunable over a broad range. We estimate the dressing using three methods: a full three-element model, an effective mode approach, and an unperturbed mode approach. The obtained results highlight the importance of the accurate estimation and control of the computational states dressing in order to optimize gates on superconducting platform.

</details>


### [2] [On quantum mechanics self-consistency: EPR incompleteness claims require no extraneous concepts beyond the theory's plain formalism for refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10006)
*D. F. Orsini,L. R. N. Oliveira,M. G. E. da Luz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从纯量子力学核心规则出发，重新审视爱因斯坦-波多尔斯基-罗森（EPR）论证，通过分析EPR态中可观测量的非对易性以及测量所获关联信息的本质，在不引入任何外部假设的前提下反驳了EPR关于量子力学不完备性的主张，证明标准量子力学形式本身足以应对这类对其物理适用范围的质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学在现象学层面极为成功，但其形式化基础的自洽性与完备性一直存在争议。EPR论证是这些基础性挑战的经典案例。虽然已有许多对EPR逻辑的批评，但这些批评通常依赖于技术性结论和对EPR解释性假设的概念性反驳，超越了量子力学自身的框架。作者希望仅基于量子力学核心规则本身来回应EPR的不完备性论证。

Method: 作者识别出EPR推理中两个关键要素：(i) EPR态中可观测量的算子总是非对易的；(ii) 测量获得的关联信息的本质。作者仅从这两点出发，严格依靠量子力学的基本规则（如测不准原理和测量公设），不援引任何额外命题或假设，系统地推导出EPR不完备性主张无法成立。

Result: 作者成功地在纯量子力学框架内推翻了EPR的不完备性论证，表明无需借助任何超出标准量子力学的额外假设，仅从其核心规则即可驳斥EPR式怀疑。这证明了标准形式主义单独就足以解决对量子理论物理适用范围的质疑。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使面对EPR这样的经典基础性挑战，标准量子力学形式本身也是自洽且结构健全的。这为量子力学作为一个内在完备的理论提供了有力的范式性证据，表明对理论不完备性的怀疑可以在其自身框架内得到解决。

Abstract: From a phenomenological and experimental viewpoint, quantum mechanics is remarkably successful in explaining effects and processes in the microscopic world. Recently, however, a growing body of literature has revisited the theory's own formal foundations, specifically addressing issues of self-consistency and completeness. As a major historical example of these foundational challenges, the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) argument famously claimed that the theory is incomplete. While many valid criticisms and refutations of the EPR logic exist, they generally rely on a combination of technical results and conceptual objections to EPR's interpretive assumptions, thus transcending the plain quantum framework itself. Motivated by these trends of examining the theory's structural limits, here we revisit the EPR argument. Focusing on elements essential to the EPR reasoning, we first analyze general quantum correlations for EPR states: (i) the fact that their observables are always associated with non-commuting operators, and (ii) the nature of the correlated information obtained through measurement. From these two points alone, and relying strictly on the core rules of quantum mechanics, we demonstrate how to overturn the alleged EPR incompleteness without invoking any extraneous propositions. Consequently, we show that the standard formalism alone suffices to resolve such type of skepticism regarding the theory's physical reach. This offers, at least in a paradigmatic instance, a powerful indication of a structurally well-founded, self-consistent quantum theory.

</details>


### [3] [When Complementary Measurements Count the Same Classical Bit Twice: Counterexamples to CQC, ECQC, and Complementarity-Based Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03828)
*Jinbo Wang,Qihang Wang,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两分支经典空检验架构，证明互补量子关联（CQC）猜想在任意维度d≥3下不成立，并进一步通过秩二经典-经典态在d=7时否证其完备基扩展（ECQC）猜想。该架构揭示了不同测量设置下读取的信息可能存在冗余，从而产生经典假阳性，并可作为多基评分赋予量子意义前的校准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互不偏置测量通常被认为能暴露量子态的不同独立方面——在一个基中经典的关联应该在互补基中消失。然而在高维空间中，不同设置下恢复的关联可能不代表不同信息，这一直觉变得微妙且有漏洞。作者旨在通过构建经典架构来揭示并利用这一漏洞，检验互补量子关联猜想的有效性。

Method: 作者提出一种两分支经典空检验架构：在测量设置选定之前，一个共享比特从两个正交乘积制备中选择其一，产生秩二的经典-经典态，然后候选协议保持不变地运行。通过数学构造证明该经典架构在不同维度下分别否证CQC猜想（所有d≥3）和ECQC猜想（d=7，且沿素数维度过冲无界增长）。该qutrit实例还被用于检验量子关联度量、单边半设备无关steering判据及条件概率猜想。

Result: 两分支经典架构在任意维度d≥3上否证了互补量子关联（CQC）猜想；一个不同的秩二经典-经典态在d=7时否证了其完备基扩展（ECQC）猜想，且过冲沿素数维度无界增长。qutrit实例对提出的量子关联度量和单边半设备无关steering判据均产生经典假阳性，并反驳了一个条件概率猜想。

Conclusion: 失败案例揭示了缺失的要求：不同设置下读取的信息必须是非冗余的。该低开销架构可作为实验和应用中的校准测试，在多基评分被赋予量子意义之前进行验证。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased measurements are commonly expected to expose independent facets of a quantum state: a correlation that is classical in one basis should disappear in a complementary basis. In higher dimensions, however, this intuition becomes particularly subtle because correlations recovered in different settings need not represent different information. To expose this loophole, we propose a two-branch classical null test: before the setting is chosen, a shared bit selects one of two orthogonal product preparations, producing a rank-two classical--classical state, and the candidate protocol then runs unchanged. Different settings can read the same bit through different outcome patterns. This two-branch classical architecture disproves the complementary-quantum correlation (CQC) conjecture in every dimension $d\geq3$. A distinct rank-two classical--classical state disproves its complete-basis extension (ECQC) at $d=7$, with an overrun that grows without bound along prime dimensions. Its qutrit CQC instance also gives classical false positives for a proposed quantum-correlation measure and a proposed one-sided semi-device-independent steering criterion, and refutes a conditional-probability conjecture. The failures identify the missing requirement: information read in different settings must be nonredundant. In experiments and applications, the same low-overhead architecture can serve as a calibration test before a multibasis score is assigned quantum meaning.

</details>


### [4] [State preparation via measurement and feedback: pushing relations, state structures, and non-invertible symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08821)
*Yabo Li,Aditi Mitra,Tsung-Cheng Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"可推动缺陷"（pushable defects）的系统性方案，用于构造一维量子态的恒定深度测量-反馈（MF）制备电路。可推动缺陷是矩阵积状态（MPS）的虚拟键算符，可在物理反馈酉操作下穿过张量。作者证明可推动缺陷及其推动关系分类了有限深度MF可制备态，并揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性（如Tambara-Yamagami对偶和余弦对称算子）之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路结合测量和酉反馈（MF）可以在恒定深度下制备长程纠缠态，但如何为给定目标态系统性构造MF制备电路仍是未充分探索的问题。

Method: 基于矩阵积状态的虚拟键算符定义"可推动缺陷"概念；将有限深度MF可制备态按照可推动缺陷及其推动关系进行分类；对每个态类关联一个参考态|B⟩，通过1轮MF电路从|B⟩制备|A⟩；当|B⟩可用有限深度局部酉电路（FDLU）制备时，|A⟩即可从积态用1轮MF电路制备；对一般目标态，迭代此过程直到相关态为FDLU可制备；对开边界MPS，该方案完备，只要有限深度MF制备（带左条件化反馈修正）可行即可构造电路。

Result: 建立了可推动缺陷与推动关系对有限深度MF可制备态的分类；给出了系统性的制备电路构造方案（开边界情形完备）；揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性的深层联系——特定推动关系类的态通过Tambara-Yamagami对偶算子或余弦对称算子（融合规则L_α L_α' = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}）与积态相关联，并可推广到非横向量子门的情形。

Conclusion: 可推动缺陷及其推动关系是统一量子态测量-反馈制备的核心原理，该表征同时揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性之间的内在联系，为量子态制备提供了系统的理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum circuits with measurements and unitary feedback (MF) can prepare long-range entangled states in constant depth, but a systematic construction of the MF preparation circuit for a given target state remains underexplored. We develop such a scheme for one-dimensional states, based on the notion of pushable defects: virtual-bond operators of a matrix product state that can be pushed through the tensor at the price of a physical feedback unitary. We show that the set of pushable defects, together with their pushing relations classifies finite-depth MF-preparable states and dictates their preparation circuits. To each class of the target state $|A\rangle$, we associate a state $|B\rangle$ from which $|A\rangle$ can be prepared using a 1-round MF circuit; in particular, $|A\rangle$ is preparable from a product state using a circuit with 1 round of MF whenever $|B\rangle$ is preparable by a finite-depth local unitary (FDLU) circuit. For a general target state, the scheme is obtained by iterating this procedure until the associated state is FDLU-preparable. For open-boundary matrix product states, the scheme is complete: it constructs a preparation circuit whenever finite-depth MF preparation with left-conditioned feedback corrections is possible. Pushable defects and pushing relations thus emerge as a unifying principle for quantum state preparation via measurements and feedback. This characterization further reveals an intrinsic connection between MF circuits and non-invertible symmetries: states with certain classes of pushing relations are related to a product state by Tambara-Yamagami duality operators, or by continuous cosine symmetry operators with fusion rules $L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}$, together with their generalizations up to (not necessarily transversal) gates.

</details>


### [5] [RF-Budgeted Frame Compilation for Frequency-Multiplexed Superconducting-Qubit Control Using Qubit-Control Identity Records and a Circuit-Informed RFSoC Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10013)
*Mingqi Ge,Anne-Marie Valente-Feliciano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向频分复用超导量子比特控制的RF预算感知的帧编译与验证工作流，该工作流结合QID记录、MATLAB/Simulink RFSoC源链模型、QuTiP qutrit动力学和Qiskit算法负载，在RF预算约束下调度多音RF帧并进行验证。模拟显示12比特BV电路在240 ns内以三个四音帧闭合，同时揭示了脉冲时长、频率映射和泄漏堆叠对旋转闭合的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频分复用的超导量子比特控制并非仅需载波分配，共享源的RF预算（包括有限带宽、峰均比、截断、量化、抖动、杂散、压缩、串扰和泄漏）会扰动多比特旋转。当前缺乏一种系统性的工作流来在电路级可验证地补偿这些RF效应。

Method: 提出RF预算化的帧编译与验证工作流：(1) QID记录编码量子比特特定的计算/泄漏跃迁频率、脉冲参数和驱动标定；(2) MATLAB/Simulink电路感知的RFSoC源链模型；(3) QuTiP qutrit动力学仿真；(4) Qiskit导出的算法电路负载。在多音RF帧调度后，将信号传播经过RFSoC模型，解码为计算和泄漏跃迁帧，最终在QuTiP中评估旋转误差、泄漏感知保真度、计算子空间存活率和瞬态泄漏。

Result: 研究表明：较长脉冲改善每帧聚合但不一定最小化时间归一化的层成本；聚类频率映射、较大旋转角和多音泄漏叠加会使闭合更紧张。在标称RF预算下，Qiskit导出的12比特BV -Y90层可在240 ns内以三个四音帧闭合；QAOA混合器分区随旋转角和脉冲时长变化。

Conclusion: 该工作流为RF受限的多比特控制提供了一种可扩展的编译-验证闭环范式，但所有结果均为基于模型的、无退相干的仿真诊断，而非实测硬件保真度或布线缩减的声明。

Abstract: Frequency-multiplexed superconducting-qubit control requires more than carrier assignment: the RF budget of a shared source can perturb multi-qubit rotations through finite bandwidth, crest factor, clipping, quantization, jitter, spurs, compression, crosstalk, and leakage. We present an RF-budgeted frame-compilation and validation workflow that combines qubit-control identity (QID) records, a MATLAB/Simulink-based circuit-informed RFSoC source-chain model, QuTiP qutrit dynamics, and Qiskit-derived algorithm workloads. QID records encode qubit-specific computational and leakage transition frequencies, pulse parameters, and drive-scale calibration, while the RF-chain profile and effective crosstalk-coupling matrix are provided as separate compiler inputs. Candidate multitone RF frames are scheduled under RF-budget constraints, propagated through the RFSoC model, decoded into computational and leakage transition frames, and evaluated in QuTiP for rotation error, leakage-aware fidelity, computational-subspace survival, and transient leakage. The studies progress from single-qutrit pulse closure to pairwise coexistence, multitone RF-frame capacity, and Bernstein-Vazirani (BV) and QAOA microwave layers extracted from Qiskit circuits. The simulations show that longer pulses improve per-frame aggregation but do not necessarily minimize time-normalized layer cost; clustered frequency maps, larger rotations, and multitone leakage stacking tighten closure. Under the nominal RF budget, a Qiskit-derived 12-qubit BV -Y90 layer closes in three validated four-tone frames at 240 ns, while QAOA mixer partitions vary with rotation angle and pulse duration. All reported results are model-based, decoherence-free simulation diagnostics rather than measured hardware fidelities or wiring-reduction claims.

</details>


### [6] [Complementary Quantum Correlations Are Universal for Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04916)
*Jinbo Wang,Qihang Wang,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了两比特（qubit）系统在互补基下测量的互信息之和不会重复计算经典关联，其总和被量子互信息所界定。这一特性源于Bloch球上二元熵的曲率，并可应用于量子信道容量下界的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从量子系统提取总关联通常需要完整态重构，但许多实验只能访问少量测量设置。一种捷径是累加互补测量得到的互信息，但在维度大于2时该过程可能重复计算相同的经典关联，因此需要确定在哪些维度下这种方法是有效的。

Method: 该证明将保护机制归结为Bloch球上二元熵的曲率，并结合了qubit信息排除权衡（information-exclusion tradeoff）与局域退相干下的数据处理不等式。

Result: 对任意两比特态，两个互补局域基下观测到的关联受测量前的量子互信息约束。由此，两个关联表即可给出无需层析的态的总关联下界；当得分超过一比特时，可定量认证单向纠缠蒸馏速率，应用于qubit信道的Choi态时，可下界估计其量子容量。

Conclusion: 该定理揭示了互补性的可操作用途，并确认了二维（qubit）是互补关联会计方法有效且可信的特殊场景。

Abstract: Extracting total correlations from a quantum system usually requires reconstructing its state, whereas many experiments access only a few measurement settings. A possible shortcut is to add the mutual informations obtained from complementary measurements; in dimensions above two, however, this procedure can count the same classical correlation twice. We establish that qubits are protected from such overcounting. For every two-qubit state, the correlations observed in two complementary local bases are bounded by the premeasurement quantum mutual information. The proof traces this protection to binary-entropy curvature on the Bloch ball and combines a qubit information-exclusion tradeoff with data processing under local dephasing. Consequently, two correlation tables give a tomography-free lower bound on total correlation. A score above one bit also certifies a quantitative one-way entanglement-distillation rate; when applied to the Choi state of a qubit channel, the same data lower bound its quantum capacity. The theorem therefore identifies both an operational use of complementarity and the trusted two-dimensional setting in which its correlation accounting is valid.

</details>


### [7] [Global Non-Identifiability of Fubini-Study Geometry from Complete One-Period Endpoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10051)
*Rashid Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对周期驱动的有限维量子系统证明了全局、最坏情形下的不可辨识性定理：即使拥有完整的、按起始时刻索引的单周期端点数据，也无法重构固定初始态的周期平均Fubini-Study度量分量。阻塞的原因为观察数据只能确定单值化（monodromy）的共轭路径，而无法确定其幺正提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在周期驱动量子系统中，一个核心问题是：完整的单周期端点数据（即从每个起始时刻出发、传播一个周期后的酉算子）能否决定系统内部的周期几何结构（如Fubini-Study度量）？先前的工作未充分厘清端点观测数据与完整周期内动力学之间的信息缺口。本文旨在精确刻画这一差距，给出确定性的全局不可辨识性结论，而非仅泛泛声称"不可观测"。

Method: 在无限制的光滑周期哈密顿量类上，系统考察起始时刻索引的单周期传播子数据。作者精确刻画了可观测纤维：观测数据确定单值化的共轭路径，但不决定其幺正提升；在固定单值化切片上，观测纤维恰好是由取值于单值化逐点中心化的光滑参数依赖闭合环生成的右轨道。由于Fubini-Study泛函在该纤维作用下不保持不变性，故无法通过端点观测映射分解。文章构造了一个实解析的两能级例子，在交换的单生成元哈密顿量族中展示该阻塞，说明无需非阿贝尔时间排序或Floquet对数歧义即可出现。还证明了在哈密顿量参数导数任意给定一致上界下不可辨识性仍然成立。

Result: 核心结果表明：周期平均的Fubini-Study度量元件的各个分量，在一般情况下不能从按每个起始时刻和外部参数索引的精确单周期传播子重构。连续性的一族哈密顿量可产生完全相同的起始时刻索引端点数据，却对应不同的（且在无限制类上可任意分离的）周期平均Fubini-Study几何。缺失的信息是观测到的单值化路径的幺正提升，而非普通的标量相位自由度。

Thus, even complete starting-time-resolved endpoint data are insufficient to determine this intra-period geometric quantity. The obstruction is characterized exactly. On each fixed-monodromy slice, the observational fibres are precisely the right orbits generated by smooth parameter-dependent based loops taking values in the pointwise centralizer of the monodromy. The result is a deterministic global statement, not a claim of generic non-identifiability or experimental impossibility. Abstract: We determine the smallest number of active questions per player at which a four-player binary exclusive-or (XOR) game of commuting-operator value one need not admit a Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) equatorial realization. Such a realization uses the four-qubit GHZ state and equatorial qubit observables, reducing perfect play to additive phase equations. We prove that every four-player XOR game with commuting-operator value one and at most three active questions per player has a perfect GHZ-equatorial strategy. Conversely, we construct a uniform eight-clause game with four active questions per player whose commuting-operator value is one but whose phase equations are inconsistent. The positive result follows by lifting every integral incidence obstruction to an ordered noncommutative refutation, using primitive circuits, forest matchings, and ternary Hamming geometry. For the separating game, a Klein four-group incidence relation obstructs the phase system, while an even-subgroup normal form and degree-one and degree-two Magnus coefficients exclude refutations of arbitrary length.

Abstract: The non-equilibrium quantum thermodynamics of many-body open systems is notoriously rich, especially in the strong-coupling regime where strong system-bath correlations develop and interactions produce non-perturbative effects. Such systems can be described by `quantum impurity models' where the system and bath are treated on an equal footing as a single composite. Paradigmatic examples are the spin-boson and Anderson impurity models, in which system degrees of freedom interact with either bosonic or fermionic gapless baths. Here we study the quantum work distribution function (WDF) of such systems following a quench. Capturing the full continuum of many-body excitations in the WDF requires a non-perturbative solution of the underlying non-equilibrium quantum impurity problem. For this purpose, we extend the time-dependent numerical renormalization group (TDNRG) approach to the calculation of the WDF, which applies directly in the thermodynamic limit, can be used for arbitrary quench amplitudes at zero or finite temperature, and provides exponentially fine low-energy resolution. Our numerically-exact solution reveals power-law threshold behavior due to the Anderson orthogonality catastrophe when the work approaches its minimum value, with universal scaling collapse of the distribution below an emergent low-energy scale induced by system-bath correlations.

Abstract: We reveal long-lived memory effects in the defect bath of a superconducting transmon qubit through electric-field tuning of two-level system (TLS) defects coupled to the qubit. The observations can be explained by TLS coupling to electric field-polarised charge fluctuators in the defect bath. This information may be used to improve qubit-stabilisation and quantum error correction protocols.

Abstract: Projected ensembles---the collections of conditional pure states induced on a system by measuring an entangled environment---have become central objects in quantum science, underlying deep thermalization, quantum state designs, the emergence of classicality, and exhibiting interesting phase transitions. Here we develop a general and exact theory of their dynamics, yielding a systematic approach to their dynamics and equilibrium and non-equilibrium stationary states. We derive an exact continuity equation on quantum state space for the projected ensemble, together with microscopic kinetic equations and analytical expressions for the probability flux and source terms generated by the system-environment interaction. The resulting dynamics admits a classical representation: isolated systems obey Hamiltonian transport and Liouville's theorem, while open systems are described by a kinetic theory of probability transport. The stationary continuity equation provides a general characterization of equilibrium and non-equilibrium stationary projected ensembles, which can be analyzed through the method of characteristics. The theory provides an analytical framework for studying the emergence, structure, and timescales of equilibrium and non-equilibrium stationary projected ensembles, with applications ranging from deep thermalization and the emergence of classicality to random quantum-state generation and quantum device benchmarking.

</details>

Method: 作者证明了在任意费米子高斯协议（含纯态输出）下，纯态马约拉纳协方差矩阵的Williamson谱被其系综平均值弱主宰。基于这一谱律，推导出费米子非平坦度（antiflatness）和占据熵等可计算非高斯性度量成为强单调量；并分析费米子宇称守恒时催化剂对主宰阻碍的不可去除性（除非催化剂携带宇称相干性），以及纯态下渐近互转换的不可逆性。

Conclusion: 该工作为费米子非高斯性提供了与纠缠理论平行的谱学基础，且所有相关量仅需从两点马约拉纳关联函数即可获取，使理论可直接在当下量子器件上作为凝聚态量子物质的实验可观测量加以检验。

Abstract: Pure-state entanglement rests on a single algebraic backbone: majorization of the Schmidt spectrum governs state conversion under local operations and classical communication, and constrains entanglement monotones. Here we establish a corresponding majorization law for fermionic non-Gaussianity, the resource that elevates free fermions to universal quantum computation. Under any fermionic Gaussian protocol with pure state outcomes, the Williamson spectrum of a pure state's Majorana covariance matrix is weakly majorized by its ensemble average. This spectral law mirrors that of entanglement theory. It turns computable non-Gaussianity quantifiers such as fermionic antiflatness and occupation entropies into strong monotones for fermionic non-Gaussianity, and delivers necessary conditions and converse bounds on state conversion under Gaussian protocols. When fermion parity is conserved, no catalyst can remove a majorization obstruction---unless it carries parity coherence---and asymptotic interconversion is irreversible already for pure states. All relevant quantities are accessible from two-point Majorana correlators, turning the theory developed here into experimentally observable properties of quantum matter, testable on present-day quantum devices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPB在量子信息中具有重要应用（如构造绑定纠缠态、研究纠缠判定等），但稳定子态集合中的类似结构尚未被系统研究。作者希望在稳定子态框架下引入不可扩展性的概念，利用辛几何工具构造并刻画这种新型的不可扩展稳定子基，从而为量子资源理论（特别是magic态资源）提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: The Uhlmann fidelity ${\rm F}(ρ_0,ρ_1) = {\rm tr}|\sqrt{ρ_0}\sqrt{ρ_1}|$ is one of the most fundamental quantities in quantum information theory for quantifying the closeness between two quantum states. Estimating the Uhlmann fidelity to within additive error $\varepsilon$ requires a number of copies of the states, or queries to their state-preparation circuits, that depends at least linearly on the smaller of the ranks of $ρ_0$ and $ρ_1$. Consequently, this rank dependence disappears when either state is pure, in which case the query and sample complexities depend only polynomially on $1/\varepsilon$. However, the known optimal estimator for ${\rm F}(ρ,|ψ\rangle\!\langleψ|)$ due to Fang and Wang (ESA 2025) requires prior knowledge of which state is pure.
  In this work, we remove this mathematically unnecessary prior-knowledge requirement and establish an optimal estimator for ${\rm F}(ρ, |ψ\rangle\!\langleψ|)$ under the sole promise that one of the two states is pure, without knowing which one. Our estimator is obtained by specializing the refined algorithmic Uhlmann transform of Utsumi, Nakata, Wang, and Takagi (2025) to the case where one state is pure. In this setting, the Uhlmann fidelity can be recovered as follows: apply a unitary dilation of ${\rm tr}_{\sf A}(|ψ_0\rangle\!\langleψ_1|)$ (or its inverse) to the reference register $\sf R$ of the purification $|ψ_1\rangle$ (or $|ψ_0\rangle$) on the registers $\sf A$ and $\sf R$, estimate the corresponding square-root amplitude in each case, and take the maximum of the resulting two estimates.

The functionality of the algorithm is verified via comparing the results obtained from state vector simulations for one- and two-dimensional test examples with their analytical solutions. For the considered test examples, it is found that the success probability for obtaining the desired ancilla qubit subspace in the LCU version is a factor of around two higher than that for the sequence of multi-controlled rotation gates. However, the LCU version requires a number of ancilla qubits up to the number of qubits that is set to store the discretized source term of the Poisson equation in amplitude encoding, whereas the sequence of multi-controlled rotation gates demands only one ancilla qubit. Computations of the success probabilities for both variants furthermore indicate that the success probability converges for a specific problem with increasing resolution. Concerning the required computational resources for the quantum algorithm, the conclusion is drawn that while the QFT is a more efficient procedure than its classical counterpart, the current implementations of the other necessary steps in the algorithm diminish the efficiency w.r.t. the runtime.

Here each dynamical decoupling operation can be viewed as an "effective field vector", whilst the phase and population of the two-level system is encoded into a "Bloch vector". The two-level dynamics are calculated by the cross product between these two vectors, such that the quantum state represented by the Bloch vector rotates around the effective field vector. Several prominent control schemes are simulated using this picture, starting with a Rabi oscillation, followed by the Ramsey sequence and a selection of pulsed and continuous dynamical decoupling techniques. These sequences are engineered to preserve control of the quantum system for as long as possible, with their efficacy often quantified by a coherence time. We clarify the definition of the longitudinal $T_1$, transverse $T_2$ and inhomogeneous $T_2^*$ coherence times, which are used across the literature. Each simulation has an accompanying animation to illustrate the protocol's two-level dynamics on the Bloch sphere. The custom simulation and Bloch sphere plotting scripts are also provided in an open access repository to allow the reader to reproduce, modify, and explore these results. Dynamical decoupling sequences have established themselves as an invaluable tool across the entire quantum technologies remit; by providing an open framework alongside this tutorial, we aim to make these protocols more accessible to new researchers discovering this thriving field.

Abstract: Quantum sensors promise measurement sensitivities that can scale at the Heisenberg limit, but in practice their performance is often degraded by noise, finite sampling, and implementation imperfections. In this work we present a general framework for improving parameter estimation in such settings by exploiting intrinsic structural constraints of time-domain correlation functions. Our approach builds on the observation of Kemper et al. [PRL 132, 160403 (2024)] that two-time correlation functions of Hermitian observables generate Gram matrices that are positive semidefinite, a property that can be violated in experimentally acquired data. We formulate signal reconstruction as a convex optimization problem that enforces positive semidefiniteness, Toeplitz structure, and low-rank priors motivated by the underlying dynamics. We show analytically that, under suitable conditions, the ground-truth signal can be uniquely identified in the noiseless case and recovered stably in the presence of noise. We further demonstrate numerically, in a GHZ-based magnetometry protocol, that enforcing these physical constraints can significantly improve frequency estimation from sparse and noisy data. In particular, we observe a clear advantage in the data-starved regime, where only a small number of time samples are available and standard spectral estimation methods, including matrix pencil techniques, provide limited or unstable improvement over direct fitting. While the reconstructed signals do not in general reach the shot-noise-limited performance, the proposed approach consistently reduces estimation error and recovers much of the underlying structure of the signal. These results indicate that incorporating universal physical constraints into data analysis can enhance the practical performance of quantum sensing protocols without requiring additional hardware resources or calibration.

Abstract: We present a full-oscillator analysis of a finite-energy GKP CHSH test whose observed score yields robust Bell-pair self-testing. Periodically binned position and momentum give the Pauli settings, while a fixed binary coarse-graining of photon number modulo four and its displaced conjugate realize the tilted settings. The large gain is specific to the deterministic phase-bit coarse-graining; independently calibrating the one-bit POVM with randomized odd-sector outcomes gives only a much smaller improvement. These are honest-model predictions, not loss, detection-efficiency, or finite-sample thresholds. In an experiment, a device-independent guarantee for an extracted Bell pair follows by inserting a confidence lower bound on the observed CHSH score into the self-testing theorem.

In addition to that, from a phenomenological point of view, one of the most intriguing results of NED theory is that photons follow null geodesics according to an effective geometry. In particular, for a two-parameter electromagnetic Lagrangian density, we might observe the vacuum birefringence phenomenon, i.e., photons can follow null geodesics according to two effective geometries. In the second, the secular precession phase is assigned to the dominant lower-order angular carriers and retained in resummed form. The latter produces a substantially stronger response because the dominant waveform components coherently accumulate the modified phase. We find that LISA can place stringent constraints on the parametrized deviation. For a binary with chirp mass $3000 M_{\odot}$, initial eccentricity $e_0=0.5$ observed for four years at an SNR of $50$, the second model yields a $90\%$ credible bound of $|δα|\lesssim 10^{-4}$. Increasing eccentricity further sharpens the constraints by introducing additional harmonic structure and reducing degeneracies among the binary parameters. The framework developed here is general and can be extended to more complete eccentric waveform models.

This parametrization captures the transition from early deceleration to late-time acceleration, with a logarithmic correction governed by $n$ that distinguishes it from standard kinematic models. Model parameters $H_0$, $q_0$, $q_1$, and $n$ are constrained via MCMC using cosmic chronometer (CC) and Pantheon+SHOES datasets, individually and in combination. Across all dataset combinations, $q_0 < 0$, confirming ongoing accelerated expansion. The reconstructed $H(z)$ is consistent with $Λ$CDM at low redshifts, with mild deviations at higher redshifts. The $\{r, s\}$ statefinder parameters indicate that the model evolves smoothly, beginning with Chaplygin gas-type behaviour, crossing the $Λ$CDM fixed point, and settling into a quintessence-like phase at late times. The $Om(z)$ diagnostic reveals negative slopes throughout, indicating quintessence-like dark energy ($w > -1$). Abstract: Newborn proto-neutron stars (PNS) are potential targets of gravitational wave asteroseismology, the study of the (inner) structure of stars via their oscillation modes. We apply this scheme to a set of 28 non-rotating 1D, 2D and 3D CCSN simulations performed with two different numerical codes and using different progenitors and equations of state. In that way, we find that there are different families of f-, p- and g-modes in the system, living in different areas. We also find that the dominant high-frequency emission mode is the f-mode of the PNS, associated with the strong density gradient at its surface. Our classification procedure is automatic, performs consistently for all the models considered, and paves the way to systematic studies of the dependence of the mode frequencies on the PNS properties, with direct application to parameter estimation from future observations of gravitational wave signals.

We find that phantom evolution requires $k\ge(3ω-1)/(2ω)$, with $ω$ the total fluid equation of state, whereas quintessence imposes the complementary upper bound. Additionally, in the exact thermal equilibrium limit, the dynamical coupling strictly enforces $k \le 2$, recovering the non-equilibrium quintessence bound. The two bounds converge to the unique value $k=2$ as the phantom divide is approached, indicating that a smooth crossing of the phantom divide is thermodynamically associated with a quadratic entropy-area scaling, where the effective theory degenerates into a logarithmic gravity framework. Finally, we demonstrate that the same thermodynamic selection principle extends to derivative-dependent cosmologies described by $f(H,\dot H)$, highlighting its robustness beyond entropy functionals that depend only on the horizon area.

Result: 混合量子化方案下等谱性破缺依然存在，偏差量级与dressed metric方法相当但略大；两种方案下等谱性破缺均随黑洞质量的立方根（普朗克单位）递减，与经典Schwarzschild极限一致；WKB方法在多数参数区域可靠，但特定模式情况下失效。

Conclusion: 等谱性破缺是圈量子引力有效黑洞几何中的普遍特征，不依赖于具体的量子化方案（dressed metric或混合方法），且其随质量增大而衰减的标度行为暗示与经典极限的平滑过渡一致。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal mode spectra of axial and polar perturbations about the effective Ashtekar, Olmedo, and Singh black hole geometry within the hybrid approach to loop quantum gravity. We also compare our results with those previously obtained by del-Corral and Olmedo for the dressed metric approach. Starting from mode equations that are straightforward effective counterparts of the classical equations, we compute these spectra by means of a high-order WKB method with Padé resummation. The violation of isospectrality between axial and polar perturbations that was claimed to exist in the dressed metric approach persists with the hybrid quantization, with deviations of the same order of magnitude as in the former effective case, although slightly larger for the hybrid prescription. In both situations, the isospectrality breaking decreases with the cubic root of the squared black hole mass in Planck units, consistently recovering the classical Schwarzschild limit. In addition, we perform a careful assessment of the applicability of the WKB approximation to the present effective analysis, confirming the parameter regions where the method remains reliable and highlighting specific mode cases for which the approximation breaks down.

</details>

*Haowen Zhong,Francesco Iacovelli,Vuk Mandic,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去的SGWB推断框架通常假设互相关估计量C̄_f服从高斯分布，但对于天体物理起源（如致密双星并合）产生的背景，这一假设从未被系统检验过。作者旨在填补这一空白，验证高斯性假设的有效性，并为下一代探测器时代提供统计基础指导。

Method: 将C̄_f分解为三个物理动机明确的组成部分：（1）平均强度，（2）几何散粒噪声，（3）偏振泄漏。通过传播局部并合率的不确定性，计算了双黑洞（BBH）、双中子星（BNS）和中子星-黑洞（NSBH）三种种群C̄_f的直到四阶累积量的90%可信区间，并分别在LIGO O5和Cosmic Explorer两种灵敏度配置下进行评估。

Result: 在LIGO O5灵敏度下，三种种群产生的C̄_f均保持有效高斯性。在CE灵敏度下，BBH产生的C̄_f呈现非高斯性，最敏感频带上一年的观测期中偏度为0.31（+0.04/-0.03），超峰度为1.3（+0.4/-0.3）；而BNS和NSBH的C̄_f即使在CE下仍大致保持高斯性。Edgeworth展开提供了对下一代时代预期非高斯性的充分领头阶描述。

Abstract: Searches for the stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) using ground-based detectors rely heavily on the cross-correlation estimator $\bar C_f$, whose statistical properties determine the theoretical foundation for inference frameworks built on it. Past analyses usually assume Gaussianity of $\bar C_f$, but this assumption has not been systematically tested for a background of astrophysical origin like the one produced by compact binary coalescences. In this work, we decompose $\bar C_f$ into three physically motivated components: (1) the mean intensity, (2) the geometrical shot noise, and (3) the polarization leakage, and we compute 90\% credible intervals for its cumulants up to fourth order for the binary black hole, binary neutron star, and neutron star-black hole populations by propagating local merger rate uncertainties. We find that for the upcoming LIGO O5 sensitivity, $\bar C_f$ remains effectively Gaussian for all three populations, validating current Gaussian likelihood frameworks. For Cosmic Explorer (CE), however, the $\bar C_f$ produced by binary black holes becomes non-Gaussian, with skewness and excess kurtosis of $0.31^{+0.04}_{-0.03}$ and $1.3^{+0.4}_{-0.3}$, respectively, in the most sensitive band over a one-year observation period. For binary neutron star and neutron star - black hole mergers, $\bar C_f$ remains largely Gaussian even at CE. These results indicate that the Edgeworth expansion provides an adequate leading-order description of the non-Gaussianity expected in the next-generation era, while also motivating the development of more sophisticated statistical methods for a more accurate treatment.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作建立了等距作用的有理同伦类与最小模型同态之间的对应关系，为计算对称空间上等距群在同伦自等价群中的包含效应提供了有效框架，且结果具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We compute the effect of the inclusion $\mathrm{F}_4 \rightarrow \mathrm{hAut}(\mathbb{O}P^2)$ on rational homotopy groups by determining the rational homotopy class of the isometry action $\mathrm{F}_4 \times \mathbb{O}P^2 \rightarrow \mathbb{O}P^2$ in terms of a homomorphism between the minimal models of source and target. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者定义了有向（参数化）拓扑复杂度的序列版本，即在给定输入空间和配置空间之间考虑有向映射的序列，构造相应的不变量。通过建立该不变量与原有不变量之间的基本关系（如不等式、函数性、纤维化性质等），并针对若干具体类别的拓扑空间（如球面、环面、可收缩空间等）进行显式计算来验证理论结果。

Result: 作者系统建立了序列式有向参数化拓扑复杂度的基本性质，完成了多类示例的显式计算。特别重要的结果是发现了该不变量对有向结构的敏感依赖性——同一底空间配备不同的有向结构（即不同的偏序或因果结构）时，该不变量可以取不同的值，说明了该不变量确实捕捉了有向动力学的信息，而不仅仅是底空间本身的拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 本文成功推广了有向（参数化）拓扑复杂度到序列情形，形成了一个既能反映有向动力学约束、又能反映参数依赖性和多阶段规划需求的统一不变量框架。计算结果进一步证实该不变量是有向结构敏感的不变量，对运动规划理论有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce sequential analogues of directed (parametrized) topological complexity, in the context of motion planning problems requiring a system to traverse a prescribed sequence of intermediate states while respecting directed dynamics and varying external parameters. We develop their basic theory, establish fundamental properties, and compute them for several classes of examples. Our computations show, in particular, that distinct directed structures on the same underlying space can have different values of this invariant.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明了Arnol'd弦猜想在一大类新的接触流形上成立：对于每个接触形式和每个闭的Legendre子流形，都存在非平凡的Reeb弦。该证明基于接触不可序性和辛化刚性的新方法，并给出了最小弦长的统一上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arnol'd弦猜想是辛几何与接触几何中的核心问题，此前仅在有限的流形类别中得到证明。作者希望将其推广到更大范围的接触流形，特别是Brieskorn流形和预量子化空间。

Method: 作者建立了接触非序性与辛化刚性之间的新联系，并将Mohnke的构造与接触Hofer几何相结合，证明了闭Legendre子流形上必然存在非平凡Reeb弦，且给出了弦长的统一上界。

Result: 证明了Arnol'd弦猜想对一大类接触流形成立，包括Brieskorn流形和大量预量子化空间；对预量子化空间在所有满足温和拓扑条件的Legendre子流形上也都成立。此外还获得了最小弦长的统一上界。

Conclusion: 通过接触Hofer几何与Mohnke构造的新联系，在更大类别上解决了Arnol'd弦猜想，为后续研究提供了新的工具和统一的上界估计。

Abstract: We prove Arnol'd's chord conjecture for a large new class of contact manifolds: for every contact form and every closed Legendrian submanifold there exists a non-constant Reeb chord with endpoints on the Legendrian. This class is characterized by contact non-orderability and rigidity of symplectizations. This proves the chord conjecture for Brieskorn manifolds, many prequantization spaces, and for all prequantization spaces under a mild topological condition on the Legendrians. Moreover, it provides a uniform upper bound on the length of the minimal chord. Our approach involves a new link between Mohnke's construction and contact Hofer geometry.

</details>


### [90] [Toric real loci via moment polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08918)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环面实轨迹是环面辛流形中的区分拉格朗日子流形，但对其拓扑性质的研究缺乏统一、直观的框架。现有的方法往往需要复杂的计算，缺少基于组合结构的简化模型。

Method: 作者引入了"万花筒"(kaleidoscope)这一多面体模型，其核心思想是考察矩映射在环面实轨迹上的限制，将环面实轨迹的拓扑问题转化为其矩多面体的组合问题。通过这一构造，建立了可定向性的判据并推导出欧拉示性数的显式公式。

Result: 该模型使得环面实轨迹的拓扑可以直接从其矩多面体的组合学中读出。作者给出了大量实例说明，并系统统计了复维数不超过9的所有光滑环面Fano簇中实轨迹可定向的情况。

Conclusion: 万花筒构造为理解环面实轨迹的拓扑提供了一个简单、可视且有效的框架，将几何问题转化为矩多面体的组合问题，具有重要的理论价值和广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Toric real loci are distinguished lagrangian submanifolds of toric symplectic manifolds. We develop a polyhedral model for toric real loci, called a kaleidoscope, based on the restriction of the moment map. This construction provides a direct geometric description of the topology of toric real loci and leads to simple criteria for orientability, together with transparent formulas for the Euler characteristic. We illustrate the theory with numerous examples and count, in every complex dimension up to 9, the smooth toric Fano varieties whose real loci are orientable. The kaleidoscope construction offers a simple, visual, and effective framework for understanding the topology of toric real loci through the combinatorics of their moment polytopes.

</details>

### [91] [Reflection Positivity in Free Fermionic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10685)
*Carl Handrack,Manfred Salmhofer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于自由费米子理论，当协方差是Dirac算子的实有理函数时，反射正性成立当且仅当所有极点都是实的、单重的且具有非负留数；同时显式展示了带指数截断因子的协方差会破坏反射正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射正性是Osterwalder--Schrader公理体系中的关键条件，它保证了从欧几里得量子场论重构出的相对论性希尔伯特空间具有正定内积。然而，对于自由费米子理论，反射正性成立的具体条件尚不清楚，特别是对于带各种截断（regulator）的协方差。这一问题的解答对于理解哪些欧几里得理论可以对应到良定义的物理理论至关重要。

Method: 作者考虑了自由费米子理论中协方差为Dirac算子D的实有理函数的情形，通过分析该有理函数极点的位置（实/复）、重数（单重/多重）以及留数的符号，给出了反射正性成立的充要条件。此外，作者对含有指数截断因子的协方差进行了显式分析，展示了其如何破坏反射正性。

Result: 得到了一个干净而精确的判据：对于实有理协方差，反射正性成立当且仅当所有极点均为实数、单重且留数非负。同时，显式构造了带指数截断因子的反例，证明这类常用的协方差不满足反射正性。

Conclusion: 这一结果给出了自由费米子理论反射正性的完全刻画，为选择合理的协方差（截断方案）提供了严格的数学指导：任何偏离"实、单重、非负留数极点"这一结构的协方差都必然破坏反射正性，从而无法重构出具备正定内积的相对论性物理理论。

Abstract: Reflection positivity is one of the Osterwalder--Schrader axioms for Euclidean quantum field theory, ensuring that the reconstructed relativistic Hilbert space carries a positive-definite inner product. For free fermionic theories whose covariance is a real rational function of the Dirac operator, we prove that reflection positivity holds if and only if all poles are real, simple, and carry non-negative residues. We also explicitly show the failure of reflection positivity for covariances with exponential regulators.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索耦合色散系统中非线性呼吸子相互作用的拓扑与几何复杂性，验证其能否自然产生分形几何与多尺度结构，为理解非线性能量局域化及流体力学的尺度相关结构提供定量框架。

Method: 首先推导KD方程的双线性形式；通过适当的辅助函数解析构造呼吸子相互作用解；采用三维体素基盒计数法计算分形维数，并辅以相对误差分析、标准误差估计、bootstrap标准差及收敛性分析以验证结果的稳健性。

Result: 呼吸波在耦合非线性环境中的相互作用产生了高度复杂的多尺度图案，在连续放大下呈现自相似行为。计算得到的分形维数为非整数，证实了图案的分形本质；误差分析和收敛性检验表明估计的分形维数具有稳健性和可重复性。

Conclusion: 非线性呼吸子相互作用可作为耦合色散系统中分形几何与复杂多尺度结构涌现的自然机制，所构建的解析与定量相结合的框架可为流体动力学、等离子体物理及非线性波传播中的能量局域化研究提供新洞见。

Abstract: Fractal structures generated through nonlinear breather interactions are investigated for the $(2+1)$-dimensional Konopelchenko--Dubrovsky (KD) equation by means of the Hirota bilinear method. The bilinear form of the system is first derived, after which breather interaction solutions are constructed analytically through suitable auxiliary functions. It is shown that the interaction of breather waves in the coupled nonlinear environment gives rise to highly intricate multiscale patterns exhibiting self-similar behaviour under successive magnification. To characterize the geometric complexity of the obtained structures, a three-dimensional voxel-based box-counting method is employed. The computed dimensions are found to be non-integer, confirming the fractal nature of the generated patterns. In addition, relative error analysis, standard error estimation, bootstrap standard deviation and convergence analysis are performed to examine the robustness and reproducibility of the estimated dimensions. The present work suggests that nonlinear breather interactions in coupled dispersive systems may provide a natural mechanism for the emergence of fractal geometries and complex multiscale structures. The combined analytical and quantitative framework developed here may provide further insight into nonlinear energy localization and scale-dependent structures arising in fluid dynamics, plasma physics and nonlinear wave propagation.

Abstract: The Renormalization Group (RG) is a powerful and versatile framework for analyzing complex physical systems. Here, we reinterpret it as a stochastic process that explores physical phase spaces, scale by scale, progressively revealing finer details of small-scale structures. This perspective establishes a natural connection to other random exploration processes, such as the Schramm-Loewner evolution, and is more suited for a probabilist audience. It links RG concepts such as RG transformations, effective actions, etc, to usual probabilistic tools such as conditional expectation values, martingales, etc, but also makes contact with stochastic quantization.

</details>

It is shown that a natural generalisation of this Lie algebra to the higher-dimensional case does not lead to a more general result because the infinite-dimensional symmetry is broken. The recently derived system, which is related to Ricci flows, is identified as a very particular case among the evolution systems obtained. All possible stationary solutions of this system in the radially symmetric case are constructed using the surprisingly rich Lie algebra of the reduced system of ODEs. Moreover, it is proved that this Lie algebra is reducible to the fifteen-dimensional algebra of the simplest system of two second-order ODEs. Several time-dependent exact solutions in the radially symmetric case are constructed as well. It is shown that the solutions obtained are bounded and smooth provided arbitrary parameters are correctly specified.

Conclusion: 该工作为因果变分原理的几何与拓扑分析提供了系统的微分演算工具，弥补了经典外微分演算在非光滑空间上的空缺，为后续研究因果结构、时空离散化及其拓扑性质奠定了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: A differential calculus for causal variational principles is developed, which generalizes the exterior calculus of differential forms and some of the associated differential topological structures to non-smooth spaces. Our calculus includes the exterior derivative, de Rham cohomology, glueing constructions (restrictions and extensions of differential forms, Mayer-Vietoris sequence), a Künneth formula and Poincar{é}'s lemma. Moreover, we prove versions of Stokes' theorem and the Gauß divergence theorem. The constructions and results are illustrated by several examples.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yamabe不等式的稳定性分析是几何分析中的重要问题。此前第二作者仅对圆球面的特殊情形进行了研究，而一般流形上的稳定性优化子存在性问题尚待解决。本文旨在推广这一结果到更一般的闭黎曼流形，并揭示不同几何情形下阈值结构的本质差异。

Method: 本文采用Aubin–Schoen检验函数的经典渐近分析方法。首先通过两个阈值条件确保稳定性优化子的存在性；然后利用爆破序列的渐近行为分析紧致性阈值，将圆球面情形与一般流形情形进行对比；最后通过正质量型条件处理维度3–5或局部共形平坦流形的互补情形。

Result: 证明了在满足两个阈值条件的闭黎曼流形上Yamabe不等式稳定性优化子的存在性。紧致性阈值由单气泡爆破序列刻画（区别于圆球面的双气泡情形）。在维度≥6且非局部共形平坦情形下，稳定性常数严格低于单气泡阈值；在互补情形下，给出了新的正质量型充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了Yamabe不等式稳定性常数与流形几何结构（维度、局部共形平坦性）之间的深刻联系，其阈值行为与Yamabe极小化子的紧致性息息相关。这项工作为后续在一般流形上研究Sobolev型不等式的精细稳定性提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We prove the existence of stability optimizers for the Yamabe inequality on closed Riemannian manifolds of dimension at least three with positive Yamabe invariant that satisfy two threshold conditions. Remarkably, the compactness threshold we uncover is different from the special case of the round sphere treated previously by the second author. More precisely, it is given by sequences blowing up in one instead of two bubbles, reflecting the compactness of Yamabe minimizers in the non-spherical case. Using the classical asymptotic analysis of Aubin--Schoen test functions, we prove that the stability constant is strictly below the one-bubble threshold in dimension at least six and when the manifold is not locally conformally flat. In the complementary case, namely in dimensions three through five or when the manifold is locally conformally flat, we find a new positive-mass-type condition which is sufficient for the strict inequality.

</details>

*Hideaki Harumoto,Kei Kondo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非负势和有限Morse指标的完备非紧Riemann 2-流形Σ的全局拓扑，证明Fischer-Colbrie度量g*的无穷远处曲率λ*_∞(Σ)完全控制该流形的全局几何与拓扑结构，包括面积增长、距离函数临界点的有界性以及端部数量的定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fischer-Colbrie的经典结果在零指标或几何稳定性假设下分类了此类流形（作为Riemann 3-流形中的浸入极小曲面）。本文旨在摆脱这两个限制性假设，仅通过Fischer-Colbrie度量在无穷远处的曲率来刻画流形的全局拓扑和几何刚性，填补了该方向上的空白。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 推导λ*_∞(Σ)与(Σ,g*)面积增长之间的基本恒等式；(2) 证明所有从固定基点p出发的距离函数d*_p的临界点被限制在有界区域内；(3) 据此获得端部数量的定量界；(4) 区分两种互补的几何视角——定量条件迫使Σ微分同胚于R²，以及单端情况下保证所有Busemann函数为穷竭函数的条件。

Result: 获得了λ*_∞(Σ)与面积增长的基本恒等式；证明距离函数的临界点限制在有界区域；作为推论获得端部的定量上界；并区分两种几何刚性态势：一种条件迫使Σ≅R²，另一种条件保证Busemann函数为穷竭函数。

Conclusion: 通过阐明距离函数临界点结构与Busemann函数无穷远全局行为这两个互补机制，展示了λ*_∞(Σ)如何作为控制流形全局几何与拓扑的核心不变量，且无需Fischer-Colbrie原始定理中的零指标或稳定性假设。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate the global topology of a complete non-compact Riemannian $2$-manifold $Σ$ admitting a Schrödinger operator with non-negative potential and finite Morse index. While classical results of Fischer-Colbrie classify such manifolds under the assumption of vanishing index or geometric stability as immersed minimal surfaces in a Riemannian $3$-manifold, we show that the curvature at infinity $λ_\infty^*(Σ)$ of the Fischer-Colbrie metric $g^*$---a complete conformal metric determined by a positive function furnished by Fischer-Colbrie's theorem---governs the global topology and geometric rigidity of $Σ$ without imposing either assumption.
  More precisely, we derive a fundamental identity relating $λ_\infty^*(Σ)$ to the area growth of $(Σ,g^*)$, show that all critical points of the distance function $d_p^*$ from a fixed base point $p$ are confined to a bounded region, and, as a corollary, obtain a quantitative bound for the number of ends. We further distinguish two complementary geometric viewpoints. On the one hand, a quantitative condition on $λ_\infty^*(Σ)$ forces $Σ$ to be diffeomorphic to the Euclidean plane $\mathbb{R}^2$. On the other hand, when $Σ$ has exactly one end, another condition on $λ_\infty^*(Σ)$ guarantees that every Busemann function on $(Σ,g^*)$ is an exhaustion. By clarifying the relationship between these two regimes---the critical-point structure of distance functions relative to a base point and the global behavior of Busemann functions at infinity---we exhibit two complementary manifestations of how $λ_\infty^*(Σ)$ controls the global geometry and topology of $Σ$.

</details>
