TL;DR: 本文对COHERENT Ge-mini和TEXONO实验中基于锗的CEνNS数据进行了全面的数值分析，检验标准模型并搜索新物理。通过联合CEνNS和EνNS信号，获得低能弱混合角的测定、中微子电磁性质的新约束、标量和矢量轻介质的互补限制，以及惰性中性轻子产生的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用最新的锗基CEνNS实验数据（COHERENT Ge-mini和TEXONO），在低能区域检验标准模型的预言，同时探索超出标准模型的新物理，包括中微子电磁性质、轻介质粒子和惰性中性轻子等。

Method: 对COHERENT Ge-mini和TEXONO实验数据进行全面的数值分析，联合CEνNS与EνNS信号，一致地处理探测器效应和系统不确定性，对弱混合角、中微子电磁性质（磁矩、毫电荷、电荷半径、环形矩）、轻标量与矢量介质子，以及经由跃迁偶极矩、标量和矢量门户产生的惰性中性轻子进行约束。

Result: 从COHERENT Ge-mini获得与SM预言一致的低能弱混合角测定；TEXONO对中微子电磁性质提供特别有竞争力的约束；包含EνNS使毫电荷灵敏度提升最多三个数量级；反应堆与停止π介子源在不同介质质量区间呈现显著互补性；对亚MeV至数十MeV质量的惰性中性轻子施加界限。

Conclusion: 当前基于锗的CEνNS实验为低能精密电弱测试和广泛超出标准模型的新物理探针提供了强大的实验平台。

Abstract: A comprehensive numerical analysis of the latest germanium based CE$ν$NS data from the COHERENT Ge-mini and TEXONO experiments has been conducted to test the Standard Model (SM) and search for new physics. By combining CE$ν$NS and E$ν$NS signals with a consistent treatment of detector effects and systematic uncertainties, we obtain a low energy determination of the weak mixing angle from COHERENT Ge-mini, in agreement with the SM prediction. We derive novel constraints on neutrino electromagnetic properties, including the magnetic moment, millicharge, charge radius, and anapole moment, with TEXONO providing particularly competitive bounds. Inclusion of E$ν$NS, significantly improves the sensitivity to the neutrino millicharge by up to three orders of magnitude. We also investigated light scalar and vector mediators, finding striking complementarity between reactor and stopped pion sources across different mediator mass regimes. Finally, we put bounds on sterile neutral leptons production through transition dipole, scalar, and vector portals, probing masses from the sub MeV to tens of MeV scale. Our results demonstrate that current germanium based CE$ν$NS experiments provide a powerful low energy laboratory for precision electroweak tests and complementary probes of a broad class of physics beyond the SM.

</details>


### [147] [Calibrated correlation between heavy-quark masses and Hadronic Vacuum Polarization observables at the precision frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10112)
*Arnau Beltran,Pere Masjuan,Antonio Rivera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，在相对论QCD求和规则中同时确定重夸克质量及其对μ子反常磁矩($a_μ$)的强子真空极化(HVP)贡献，利用二者之间的反相关性收紧不确定性，并给出粲和底夸克扇区的NLO结果与首个格点QCD计算吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: μ子反常磁矩的理论预言依赖于强子真空极化(HVP)，而色散方法与格点QCD之间的张力尚未解决。作者注意到重夸克质量与其HVP贡献并非独立量，二者源于同一强子谱函数的积分，仅积分核不同。常规做法将二者分开处理，无法利用其内在关联，且残余的理论/模型依赖性（如对偶破坏、连续谱建模）得不到直接诊断。因此需要一种自洽的统一框架来同时确定这两个量。

Method: 打破传统单一求和规则的概念，将积分核视为QCD求和规则中的自由选择，在相对论QCD求和规则的框架内同时求解重夸克质量及其对$a_μ$的HVP贡献。利用两物理量间的反相关性来锐化最终不确定性，并将微扰描述与强子描述之间的残余不一致直接转化为具体的可观测诊断量，以评估对偶破坏与连续谱模型效应。

Result: 得到粲和底夸克在领头阶的HVP贡献$a_μ^{\rm HVP_{c+b},LO}=(14.46(13)+0.3009(17))\times 10^{-10}$，次领头阶贡献$a_μ^{\rm HVP_{c+b}, NLO_{a,b}}=(-0.5738(95)-0.01822(13))\times 10^{-10}$。NLO结果与首个格点QCD计算在粲扇区吻合良好。同时得到致密质量$\hat m_c(\hat m_c)=1267.1(6.8)$ MeV和$\hat m_b(\hat m_b)=4182.3(7.2)$ MeV，具有前所未有的唯象学精度。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，重夸克质量与HVP贡献可以在一个自洽的QCD求和规则框架内同时确定，通过利用二者相关性可获得更紧密的不确定性，并为理论/模型依赖提供直接的可观测量特定诊断。粲扇区NLO结果与格点QCD的一致性增强了该方法的可靠性，并为解决$a_μ$中HVP张力提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The theoretical prediction of the muon anomalous magnetic moment $a_μ$ depends crucially on the Hadronic Vacuum Polarization (HVP), and the tension between its dispersive and lattice-QCD determinations remains unresolved. We show that part of this puzzle can be addressed in the heavy-quark sector, where both descriptions are theoretically clean, by recognizing that the heavy-quark mass and its contribution to $a_μ$ are not independent quantities: both follow from integrals of the same hadronic spectral function, differing only in their integration kernel. Promoting this kernel to a free choice within the relativistic QCD Sum Rules used to determine heavy-quark masses, we break with the conventional notion of a single valid sum rule and instead determine the mass and its HVP contribution simultaneously, from a common, self-consistent framework. This intrinsic construction exploits the anticorrelation between the two quantities to sharpen the final uncertainty, and turns the residual disagreement between the perturbative and hadronic descriptions of the observable into a direct observable-specific diagnostic of residual theory/model dependence, including duality-violation and continuum-modeling effects, unavailable to a determination of the mass alone. We obtain $a_μ^{\rm HVP_{c+b},LO} =(14.46(13)+0.3009(17))\times 10^{-10}$ at leading and $a_μ^{\rm HVP_{c+b}, NLO_{a,b}} = ( -0.5738(95) - 0.01822(13) )\times 10^{-10}$ at next-to-leading order, for charm and bottom contributions, respectively. We compare our next-to-leading-order results with its first available lattice determination, finding good agreement in the charm sector. As a byproduct, we obtain $\hat m_c(\hat m_c)=1267.1(6.8)$ MeV and $\hat m_b(\hat m_b) = 4182.3(7.2)$ MeV, with unprecedented phenomenological precision.

</details>


### [148] [Heavy neutral leptons beyond the BBN bound: probing the lepton asymmetry of the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10123)
*Kensuke Akita,Maksym Ovchynnikov,Vsevolod Syvolap*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种大轻子味不对称性方案，使重中性轻子（HNL）通过π+和π-的不对称注入规避大爆炸核合成（BBN）对长寿粒子的排除限制，从而打开SHiP实验可达到的参数空间，并可通过BBN、CMB及引力波观测联合确定宇宙的轻子不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBN对寿命τ≳0.02秒的强子衰变粒子几乎独立于丰度地施加严格排除约束（π介子衰变将质子转为中子导致氦过产生），这覆盖了即将进行的加速器实验（如SHiP）本可达到的HNL耦合参数空间。作者旨在寻找一种规避该BBN限制的物理场景，使加速器搜索的真实灵敏度得以释放。

Method: 提出具有大轻子味不对称性的场景：Dirac型HNL衰变为π+而其反粒子衰变为π-；通过两种粒子数目的不对称性，注入过量的π+将中子恢复为标准丰度，同时利用不对称性与HNL衰变之间的抵消效应将N_eff的偏移保持微小。该机制适用于m_l+m_π≲m_N≲1 GeV且τ_N≲1 s的参数范围。

Result: 该方案打开了与π和电子质量之和至1 GeV范围内、寿命短于1秒的HNL参数空间，其中包含SHiP实验可探测的实质性区域。在该区域的发现结合BBN和CMB观测量，可将原初轻子味不对称性固定至仅剩一个自由方向；该方向可被宇宙残余中微子背景以及可能的一阶宇宙QCD相变（引力波源）探测。

Conclusion: 轻子味不对称性提供了一种自然机制使HNL规避BBN限制，从而恢复加速器实验中可观测量。该方案使得SHiP发现、BBN/CMB观测与中微子背景/引力波探测的结合能够完整确定宇宙的轻子不对称性，为理解宇宙物质-反物质不对称性提供了新的多信使途径。

Abstract: Hadronically decaying particles with lifetimes $τ\gtrsim0.02\,{\rm s}$ are excluded by Big Bang Nucleosynthesis almost independently of their abundance: the mesons from their decays convert protons into neutrons faster than the reverse, and helium is overproduced. For heavy neutral leptons (HNLs), this blankets the couplings the upcoming accelerator searches can reach. We propose a scenario with large lepton flavor asymmetries in which HNLs evade it. A Dirac HNL decays into $π^+$ and its antiparticle into $π^-$, so an asymmetry between the two populations injects an excess of $π^+$, which converts the neutrons back to their standard abundance, while a cancellation between the asymmetries and the HNL decays keeps the shift in $N_{\rm eff}$ small. This opens the parameter space with $m_l+m_π\lesssim m_N\lesssim1\,{\rm GeV}$ and $τ_N\lesssim1\,{\rm s}$, including a substantial region within the reach of SHiP. A discovery there, combined with the BBN and CMB observables, would fix the primordial flavor asymmetries up to one remaining direction. The relic neutrino background and a possibly first-order cosmic QCD transition, a source of gravitational waves, may probe that direction, and with it the lepton asymmetry of the Universe.

</details>


### [149] [Scalar baryons in neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10141)
*Julian Heeck,Yu Zhi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了携带重子数的GeV尺度标量粒子对中子星物态方程的影响。发现需要非微扰级别的排斥性自耦合才能支撑观测到的两倍太阳质量中子星；而具有吸引性自相互作用的势能甚至可以在标量质量超过化学势时触发标量产生，类似于Coleman的Q物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的重子化学势可以超过中子质量，这为将中子转化为携带重子数的新粒子提供了有利条件——即使这些过程在真空中是被运动学禁止的。研究这类新粒子对中子星性质的影响有助于揭示高密度核物质中的新物理。

Method: 研究中子星中的GeV尺度携带重子数的标量粒子，分析其对物态方程的影响。分别考察具有排斥性自耦合和吸引性自相互作用的两种势能形式，其中吸引性情况与Coleman的Q物质相似。

Result: 发现要支撑观测到的两倍太阳质量中子星，需要非微扰级别大的排斥性自耦合常数；吸引性自相互作用势能可以允许标量产生甚至当质量高于化学势时，产生了Coleman Q物质类似的物理情景。

Conclusion: GeV尺度携带重子数的标量粒子对中子星物态方程有显著影响，排斥性耦合需要非微扰强度来维持中子星质量，而吸引性势能则开辟了新的物质形态可能性，与Q物质类比。

Abstract: Neutron stars have baryon chemical potentials that can exceed the neutron mass, providing favorable conditions to convert neutrons to new particles carrying baryon number, even if those processes are kinematically forbidden in vacuum. We study the effect of GeV-scale scalars with baryon number on neutron stars' equation of state and show that non-perturbatively large repulsive self-couplings are required to support the observed two-solar-mass neutron stars. We also study potentials with attractive self-interactions, which can trigger scalar production even for masses above the chemical potential and resemble Coleman's Q-matter.

</details>


### [150] [Connecting Heavy-Quarkonium Born-Oppenheimer EFT to the Peskin OPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10201)
*Arkadiy I. Syamtomov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个从源依赖的QCD到BOEFT（Born-Oppenheimer有效场论）的匹配框架，用于描述紧致重夸克偶素的胶子响应。通过规范协变的投影响应区分动力学的BO道和由局域逆矩表示的能隙道，建立了通道因子化局域OPE的充分条件，并在弱耦合与源能隙层级下将其约化为Peskin构造。在库仑极限下恢复了Bhanot-Peskin电矩与解离截面，并作为磁扩展给出了自旋单态1S态的M1逆矩和协变动能海鸥项对磁匹配系数的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克偶素在胶子介质中的响应是理解其解离、热化和谱学性质的关键。传统的Peskin构造在弱耦合框架下已被广泛使用，但其适用范围和更高阶（特别是磁性）修正的系统性处理尚不清晰。作者旨在建立一个更通用的、源依赖的QCD到BOEFT匹配框架，统一处理电性和磁性胶子响应，并明确区分动态保留的BO道与积掉的能隙道的贡献。

Method: 采用源依赖的QCD到BOEFT匹配方法。核心工具包括：(1) 规范协变的投影响应，用于分离动态保留的BO道和由局域逆矩表示的能隙道；(2) 传播子-顶点界（propagation-vertex bounds）作为通道因子化局域OPE的充分条件；(3) 通过弱耦合层级与源能隙层级将广义构造约化为Peskin构造；(4) 在库仑极限下从单一谱测度恢复电矩与解离截面；(5) 计算磁扩展项，包括自旋单态1S态的序贯M1逆矩和协变动能海鸥项。

Result: 建立了源依赖的QCD-to-BOEFT匹配框架，给出了局域OPE成立的充分条件，并在适当层级下恢复Peskin构造和Bhanot-Peskin结果。对于磁扩展，自旋单态1S态的各向同性序贯M1逆矩给出c_{B,GG}^{(1)ij}=5πα_s^2(c_FV_{iso}^{(s)})^2δ^{ij}/16，领先协变动能海鸥项给出c_{B,dia}^{(1)ij}=-πα_s^2δ^{ij}/4。这些是磁匹配系数的可辨识贡献而不是完整值。

Conclusion: 该工作为重夸克偶素胶子响应提供了一个系统性的、超越Peskin构造的BOEFT匹配方案，能够同时容纳电性与磁性响应，并为后续更高阶计算和数值应用奠定了基础。磁匹配系数的完整值需要进一步包含其他贡献。

Abstract: We construct a source-dependent QCD-to-BOEFT matching for the gluonic response of compact heavy quarkonium. A gauge-covariant projected response separates BO channels retained dynamically from gapped sectors represented by local inverse moments. Propagation-vertex bounds provide sufficient criteria for a channel-factorized local OPE, while the additional weak-coupling and source-gap hierarchies reduce this response to the Peskin construction. In the Coulombic limit the established Bhanot-Peskin electric moments and dissociation cut arise from one spectral measure. As a magnetic extension, for a spin-singlet $1S$ state the isotropic sequential $M1$ inverse moment gives $c_{B,GG}^{(1)ij}=5πα_s^2 (c_FV_{\rm iso}^{(s)})^2δ^{ij}/16$, while the leading covariant-kinetic seagull gives $c_{B,{\rm dia}}^{(1)ij}=-πα_s^2δ^{ij}/4$. These are identifiable contributions to, rather than the complete value of, the magnetic matching coefficient.

</details>


### [151] [Extraction of baryon number susceptibilities at finite density from heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10238)
*Grégoire Pihan,Roman Poberezhniuk,Volodymyr A. Kuznietsov,Volodymyr Vovchenko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次从重离子碰撞的质子数累积量数据中，利用贝叶斯方法提取了有限重子密度下QCD物质的重子数磁化率。通过将任意状态方程磁化率嵌入最大熵冻结动力学粒子化超曲面，并考虑实验运动学接受度和严格重子数守恒，作者从RHIC束流能量扫描数据中确定了χ₂^B（与格点QCD在μ_B≲300 MeV时定量一致），并发现更大μ_B下重子数涨落相对非临界格点外推有所增强。高阶磁化率约束较弱；质子阶乘累积量比C₃/C₁的非单调碰撞能量依赖性可由χ₂^B的能量依赖与严格重子数守恒的相互作用描述，无需三体或四体重子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限重子密度下从实验数据直接提取QCD磁化率是一个长期挑战。此前的主要信息来源是格点QCD模拟，但其在有限化学势下受符号问题限制；而实验测量质子数涨落与理论量之间的映射关系复杂，缺乏系统的、包含精确守恒律和实验接受度的框架。作者旨在弥合理论热力学量与实验可观测量之间的鸿沟。

Method: 该框架将任意状态方程磁化率χ_n^B嵌入最大熵冻结的流体动力学粒子化超曲面，通过将(反)重子涨落映射到质子，应用实验运动学接受度，并严格计入重子数守恒。随后对RHIC束流能量扫描0-5%中央Au-Au碰撞中(net-)质子数涨落测量进行贝叶斯推断，逐碰撞能量提取χ₂^B/χ̄₂^B、χ₃^B/χ₁^B和χ₄^B/χ₂^B。

Result: χ₂^B获得强约束，在μ_B≲300 MeV时与沿化学冻结线的格点QCD估计定量一致；更大μ_B下显示相对非临界格点外推和HRG基线增强的重子数涨落。χ₃^B和χ₄^B约束较弱。关键发现：实验观测的质子阶乘累积量比C₃/C₁的非单调能量依赖可无需高阶不可约重子关联即可复现，源于χ₂^B能量依赖与精确重子数守恒的共同作用。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了从重离子碰撞数据系统提取QCD热力学涨落性质的可行框架，为探测QCD相结构和临界涨落信号提供了新途径。χ₂^B在低μ_B区域与格点QCD一致验证了方法的可靠性；高μ_B区域的增强可能暗示偏离HRG模拟背景的物理。高阶磁化率的弱约束表明需要在更高阶累积量和/或更多系统能量上提高实验精度。

Abstract: We present, to our knowledge, the first Bayesian extraction of baryon number susceptibilities of QCD matter at finite baryon density from heavy-ion collision data on proton number cumulants. The framework embeds arbitrary equation-of-state susceptibilities $χ_n^B$ into realistic hydrodynamic particlization hypersurfaces through maximum-entropy freeze-out, maps the resulting (anti)baryon fluctuations onto protons, applies the experimental kinematic acceptance, and accounts for exact baryon number conservation. Applying the framework to measurements of (net-)proton number fluctuations in 0--5\% central Au-Au collisions from the RHIC Beam Energy Scan in the collider mode, we extract, at each collision energy, the second-order susceptibility normalized by the hadron resonance gas value, $χ_{2}^B/\barχ_{2}^B$, and the higher-order susceptibility ratios $χ_{3}^B/χ_{1}^B$ and $χ_{4}^B/χ_{2}^B$. We obtain tight constraints on $χ_{2}^B$, with extracted values in quantitative agreement with lattice QCD based estimates along the chemical freeze-out line for $μ_B \lesssim 300$~MeV. At larger $μ_B$, the extracted values indicate an enhancement of baryon number fluctuations relative to the noncritical lattice-based extrapolation and HRG baseline considered here. The third- and fourth-order susceptibilities are only weakly constrained. In particular, we find that the observed nonmonotonic collision-energy dependence of the proton factorial cumulant ratio $\hat{C}_{3}/\hat{C}_{1}$ can be described without irreducible three- or four-baryon correlations. This behavior emerges from the interplay between the energy dependence of $χ_{2}^B$ and exact baryon number conservation.

</details>


### [152] [The near-threshold cross section of $e^+e^- \to Ω^-\barΩ^+$: Heavy-flavor rescattering and physics-informed deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10301)
*Sara Rahmani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII对e+e-→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺截面的精密测量数据，构建包含ΩΩ̄、ΞΞ̄和D_sD̄_s相互作用的耦合道框架，并采用两步机器学习方法（模拟推断+物理信息神经网络）研究D_sD̄_s阈值附近的结构，发现一个位于3.847 GeV、零衰变宽度的束缚态极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BESIII合作组对e+e-→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺截面的高精度测量数据，探索D_sD̄_s阈值附近的潜在强子重散射结构，这是理解非微扰量子色动力学中奇异重子对产生和阈值动力学的重要机会。

Method: 构建包含ΩΩ̄、ΞΞ̄和D_sD̄_s的耦合道框架，从有效拉氏量（尊重重夸克自旋对称性、手征对称性和隐局域对称性）出发推导驱动势，通过Bethe-Salpeter方程的壳上因子化实现散射振幅的幺正化。采用两步机器学习框架：(1) 基于混合密度网络的模拟推断映射全局贝叶斯后验；(2) 利用柯西-黎曼物理信息神经网络（PINN）在多个黎曼片上平滑延拓振幅到复能量平面，避免传统求根方法的不稳定性。

Result: 在复能量平面上隔离出一个位于M=3.847 GeV、衰变宽度为零的极点，位于D_s⁻D̄_s⁺阈值以下89 MeV处。对应的S矩阵留数显示该极点主要耦合到D_sD̄_s道，表明阈值动力学由动力学产生的D_s⁻D̄_s⁺束缚态所驱动。

Conclusion: 在D_sD̄_s阈值以下存在一个由动力学产生的束缚态，该态由强子间的耦合重散射机制形成，贡献了观测到的阈值附近结构。

Abstract: Recent precision measurements of the $e^+e^- \to Ω^-\barΩ^+$ cross section by the BESIII collaboration provide a valuable opportunity to probe complex hadronic rescattering mechanisms. In this work, we investigate a potential structure near the $D_s\bar{D}_s$ threshold using a coupled-channel framework incorporating $Ω\barΩ$, $Ξ\barΞ$, and $D_s\bar{D}_s$ interactions. The driving potentials are derived from effective Lagrangians respecting heavy quark spin symmetry, chiral symmetry, and hidden local symmetry, and the scattering amplitude is unitarized via the on-shell factorization of the Bethe-Salpeter equation. To go beyond local fits and map theoretical uncertainties, we use a two-step machine-learning framework. First, Simulation-Based Inference with a Mixture Density Network maps the global Bayesian posterior of the effective couplings. Second, to identify the non-perturbative threshold dynamics without the instabilities of traditional root-finding across multiple Riemann sheets, we employ a Cauchy-Riemann Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN). The network enforces mathematical analyticity, smoothly continuing the real-axis amplitude into the complex energy plane. We isolate a pole at $M = 3.847$ GeV with zero decay width, sitting $89$~MeV below the $D_s^-\bar{D}_s^+$ threshold. The corresponding S-matrix residues show an overwhelming coupling to the $D_s\bar{D}_s$ channel, indicating that the threshold dynamics are driven by a dynamically generated $D_s^-\bar{D}_s^+$ bound state.

</details>


### [153] [Commensurate Structure of Quark and Lepton Mixing: Eighteenth Powers of One Parameter and a Digital-Clock Quantization of the Unitarity Triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10312)
*Vernon Barger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在夸克和轻子混合数据中识别出两种精确的对应结构——乘法结构与角度结构——并揭示其精确关系。基于层次参数ε=|V_ub|^{3/10}，费里茨希-邢（FX）角被精确表达为ε的简单幂次，相位为最大相位；其重相位不变图像是一个在π/24格点上量子化的幺正三角形（数字时钟），轻子PMNS矩阵在相同格点上预言δ_CP≈296°或244°，且同一ε还设定中微子质量比r的幂次关系，所有关系均与实验数据高度一致，全部在参数化层次进行，不引入任何动力学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找夸克与轻子味混合数据背后的共同结构规律，试图用统一的数学框架（乘法结构+角度结构）精确描述CKM和PMNS矩阵的混合参数，并揭示从夸克到轻子的对应关系。

Method: 利用层次参数ε≡|V_ub|^{3/10}表达FX角的幂次规律；通过幺正三角形的π/24格点量子化建立数字时钟结构（α,β,γ)=(12,3,9)×7.5°；利用列1与列3构造不含马约拉纳相位的PMNS角度模拟；用同一ε参数化中微子质量比r≡m_2/m_3=ε^{19/18}并推导PMNS第一行的幂次表达式。所有分析限定在参数化层次，无动力学假设。

Result: FX角sinθ_u=ε^{26/18}、sinθ_d=ε^{17/18}、sinθ=ε^{34/18}系数偏差在2.5%以内；相位φ_FX=92.3°±2.7°接近π/2；Jarlskog不变量J=ε^{111/18}sinφ_FX与测量值3.08(14)×10^{-5}匹配；幺正三角形在π/24格点上满足(α,β,γ)=(90°,22.5°,67.5°)且1σ一致；轻子预言δ_CP≈296°或244°；中微子质量比r=ε^{19/18}及PMNS第一行幂次关系均与数据符合在0.2σ水平。

Conclusion: 夸克与轻子混合数据中存在统一的精确结构：乘法（幂次）结构与角度（格点量子化）结构通过同一层次参数ε紧密关联。夸克和轻子幺正三角形共享β=22.5°角，仅相差两个格点单位的转移。该框架提出了一系列可检验的预言（δ_CP、中微子质量比、PMNS第一行），有待DUNE、Hyper-Kamiokande和JUNO实验验证。论文明确将分析限定在参数化层面，不做任何动力学假设。

Abstract: We identify two commensurate structures in the measured quark and lepton mixing data, one multiplicative and one angular, and their exact relation. With the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon\equiv|V_{ub}|^{3/10}=0.1869\pm0.0018$, the measured Fritzsch--Xing (FX) angles satisfy $\sinθ_u=\varepsilon^{26/18}$, $\sinθ_d=\varepsilon^{17/18}$, and $\sinθ=\varepsilon^{34/18}$ with coefficients within $2.5\%$ of unity, and the phase is maximal, $φ_{\rm FX}=92.3^\circ\pm2.7^\circ$ against $π/2$. Unit coefficients reproduce the remaining moduli at the percent level and fix the derived Jarlskog invariant, $J=\varepsilon^{111/18}\sinφ_{\rm FX}$, matching the measured $3.08(14)\times10^{-5}$. The rephasing-invariant image of this structure is a unitarity triangle quantized on the $π/24$ lattice, a digital clock, $(α,β,γ)=(12,3,9)\times7.5^\circ$, consistent with data at $1σ$ through $α\simeqφ_{\rm FX}$ and the theorem $\tanβ\simeq\sqrt{\varepsilon}$; degree-level $γ$ confronts $67.5^\circ$--$68^\circ$. For the leptons, where only the angular structure can act, its PMNS analog, formed from columns $1$ and $3$, free of Majorana phases, on the same lattice requires $δ_{\rm CP}\simeq296^\circ$ in the upper octant or $244^\circ$ in the lower, testable at DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande. The quark and lepton triangles share $β=22.5^\circ$, measured for quarks, predicted for leptons, and differ by a transfer of two lattice units, with corollary $δ_{\rm CP}\simeq-δ\simeq295^\circ$. The same $\varepsilon$ sets the neutrino mass ratio, $r\equiv m_2/m_3=\varepsilon^{19/18}$, and the PMNS first row follows in powers of $r$, $(r^{1/9},\,r^{1/3},\,\tfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}r)$, both relations at $0.2σ$ and testable at JUNO. The analysis is confined to the parameterization level; no dynamics is assumed.

</details>


### [154] [Event-by-event analysis of chiral charge separation in $p^{\uparrow}+$Au collisions within an improved AMPT model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10496)
*Chen Gao,Gui-Zhen Wu,Yi Xu,Wei-Tian Deng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在改进的弦融化AMPT模型中研究了$p^{\uparrow}$+Au碰撞在$\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200$ GeV下由手征磁效应（CME）引起的电荷分离。通过引入碰撞参数依赖的formation-time延迟降低峰值Bjorken能量密度，并复现PHENIX测得的$v_2(p_T)$。发现初态关联器追踪$B^2$具有清晰的分级层次，部分子级联保留约80-90%的信号，而凝聚和ART强子散射耗散大部分信号，最终态仅保留约10-30%。$\gamma_{OS}/\gamma_{SS}$分裂仍然可见，支持将$\Delta\gamma_{IV}-\Delta\gamma_{II}$间方案差异作为本质上无背景的CME观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证改进的AMPT模型中CME信号在$p$+Au碰撞中的可观测性，并检验所提出的$\Delta\gamma_{IV}-\Delta\gamma_{II}$无背景CME观测量方案的有效性。

Method: 采用改进的弦熔化AMPT模型进行逐事例模拟，引入碰撞参数依赖的formation-time延迟以降低峰值能量密度；CME源逐事例实现，其夸克动量交换分数与$|\mathbf{B}|_{\mathrm{event}}/|\mathbf{B}|_{\mathrm{max}}$成正比并在7%处截断；通过部分子级联、凝聚和ART强子重散射全过程追踪信号演化。

Result: 初态关联器随$B^2$呈现清晰的方案层次；部分子级联保留80-90%的CME信号，但凝聚和强子重散射使最终态仅保留10-30%；尽管信号大量耗散，$\gamma_{OS}/\gamma_{SS}$分裂仍然可见。

Conclusion: $p$+Au碰撞中CME信号虽然显著衰减但仍可观测，$\Delta\gamma_{IV}-\Delta\gamma_{II}$间方案差异可作为本质上无背景的CME探针，为该观测量在实验中的应用提供理论支持。

Abstract: We study the charge separation induced by chiral magnetic effect (CME) in $p^{\uparrow}$+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200$ GeV event-by-event with an improved string-melting AMPT model. In this model, an impact-parameter-dependent formation-time delay is introduced to reduce the peak Bjorken energy density from $\sim$2 to $\sim$0.3 GeV/fm$^3$ and reproduces the illiptic flow $v_2(p_T)$ measured by PHENIX in $p+$Au simultaneously. The event-by-event CME source, whose quark momentum-exchange fraction scales with $|\mathbf{B}|_{\mathrm{event}}/|\mathbf{B}|_{\mathrm{max}}$ and is capped at the 7\% value from Au+Au, transmits the field geometry directly to the observable. We find that the initial correlator shows a clear scheme hierarchy tracking $B^2$. The parton cascade preserves $\sim$80--90\% of the signal, while coalescence and ART hadronic rescattering dissipate the bulk, leaving $\sim$10--30\% in the final state. The $γ_{OS}/γ_{SS}$ splitting remains visible. These results support the use of the inter-scheme difference $Δγ_{\mathrm{IV}}-Δγ_{\mathrm{II}}$ as an essentially background-free CME observable as we proposed in previous work.

</details>


### [155] [Nucleon electromagnetic form factors in nonlocal chiral effective theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10543)
*Y. Salamu,Alim Ablat*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在非局域框架下计算了核子电磁形状因子到O(p^4)阶的π介子单圈修正，并纳入领头阶核子-矢量介子耦合，通过重新拟合手征低能常数，显著改善了大Q^2区域电磁形状因子的动量依赖性，使其与实验数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前局域分析框架下核子电磁形状因子在大Q^2区域的动量依赖性描述不够理想，需要系统地研究非局域框架及矢量介子主导效应对形状因子的改进作用。

Method: 在非局域（非定域）有效场论框架下，计算核子电磁形状因子的π介子单圈修正至O(p^4)阶；同时引入领头阶核子-矢量介子耦合以系统考察矢量介子主导的贡献。由于手征低能常数（LECs）存在重整化方案依赖性，作者将这些常数重新拟合至核子磁矩、电磁半径及电磁形状因子的Q^2依赖性实验数据。

Result: 与先前的局域分析结果相比，非局域框架下的模型在大Q^2区域对电磁形状因子Q^2依赖性的描述显著改善，且与实验数据一致。

Conclusion: 非局域框架有效改善了核子电磁形状因子在大动量转移区域的行为，验证了非局域化方案及矢量介子耦合在核子电磁性质描述中的重要作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the pion one-loop corrections to the electromagnetic form factors of the nucleon up to $\mathcal{O}(p^4)$ order in the nonlocal framework. To systematically investigate the role of vector-meson dominance in the model, we also include the leading-order nucleon-vector-meson coupling. Owing to the renormalization-scheme dependence of chiral low-energy coupling constants (LECs), we refit these constants to the nucleon magnetic moments, electromagnetic radii, and the $Q^2$-dependence of the electromagnetic form factors. Compared with the previous local analysis, the model results show that, in the nonlocal framework, the $Q^2$-dependence of the electromagnetic form factors in the large-$Q^2$ region is significantly improved and are consistent with experimental data.

</details>


### [156] [Probing vorticity and fluctuations in a rotating hadron resonance gas at LHC energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10561)
*S. Ipsita Sahoo,Sanjeebani Biswal,D. Dutta,D. K. Mishra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG)模型框架下研究了非中心超相对论重离子碰撞中产生的大涡度对强子产率的影响。结果显示旋转显著改变强子产率及其比率，增强p/π+比值而抑制K+/π+比值，并以此作为旋转的敏感探针。通过与ALICE实验数据比较，估计了不同中心度下冻结时刻的最大涡度(外围碰撞约0.088 GeV,中心碰撞约0.028 GeV)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非中心重离子碰撞中产生的大涡度会在部分子和强子物质中诱导有效化学势，影响夸克-强子相变及相关性质。研究旋转对强子物质热力学性质的影响，特别是量化涡度对可观测量的影响，对于理解旋转强子物质的性质及其现象学后果至关重要。

Method: 采用Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG)模型，引入旋转（涡度）作为有效化学势来研究强子产率及其比值的变化。计算p/π+和K+/π+比值对涡度的依赖关系，并与ALICE合作组在Pb+Pb碰撞√sNN=5.02 TeV下测量的p/π比值的中心度依赖进行比较，从而估算冻结时刻的最大涡度。

Result: 旋转显著修正强子产率及其比值：旋转增强p/π+比值而抑制K+/π+比值。通过与ALICE实验数据对比，估计外围碰撞的冻结涡度上限约为0.088 GeV，中心碰撞约为0.028 GeV。此外还研究了旋转对守恒量涨落及其关联的影响。

Conclusion: p/π+和K+/π+比值可作为重离子碰撞中产生介质的旋转性质的敏感探针。该研究为评估旋转在强子物质热力学中的作用以及重离子碰撞的现象学后果提供了定量框架。

Abstract: A large vorticity produced in non-central ultra-relativistic heavy-ion collisions induces an effective chemical potential in both partonic and hadronic matter, thereby influencing the quark-hadron transition and its associated properties. In this work, we investigate the influence of rotation on hadron yields within the framework of Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG) model. Our results show that vorticity significantly modifies hadron yields and their ratios. Most notably, rotation enhances the $p/π^+$ ratio while suppressing the $K^+/π^+$ ratio, suggesting that these observables may serve as sensitive probes of the rotational properties of the medium created in heavy-ion collisions. To quantify the effect of rotation, the calculated dependence of the $p/π^+$ ratio on vorticity is compared with the centrality dependence of the $p/π$ ratio measured by the ALICE collaboration in Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{_{NN}}}$ = 5.02 TeV. From this comparison, we estimate the maximum vorticity produced at freeze-out for different collision centralities. In case of peripheral collisions, the freeze-out vorticity ($ω$) is found to reach an upper bound value of approximately 0.088 GeV, whereas for central collisions it is about 0.028 GeV. Furthermore, we investigate the effect of rotation on fluctuations of conserved quantities and their correlations. This study provides a quantitative framework for assessing the role of rotation in the thermodynamics of hadronic matter and its phenomenological consequences for heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [157] [Probing Gluon Linear Polarization with Dihadron Fragmentation in $χ_b$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10570)
*Zhi-Guo He,Guanghui Li,Yang Liu,Yu-Jie Tian,Xin-Kai Wen,Bin Yan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用P波底偶素态χ_b0的颜色单态衰变通道，通过产生两个能量强子（具有关联的线性极化gluon）的分裂来首次直接探测线性极化胶子的双强子碎裂函数（DiFF），并同时通过半单举衰变率约束非极化胶子DiFF。基准模型预测不对称度为百分量级，现有Belle数据可能达到，而Belle II数据可显著提高统计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性极化胶子的双强子碎裂函数（DiFF）至今未被实验探测到，这是自旋依赖胶子强子化领域的一个重要空白。作者旨在寻找第一个可直接探测该量的物理通道。

Method: 在领头阶下，利用P波底偶素态χ_b0的颜色单态衰变通道，该衰变产生两个具有关联线性极化的能量胶子。在共线因子化框架内，这两个胶子分别碎裂为独立的双强子对，从而产生Artru-Collins型角关联，为线性极化胶子DiFF提供直接探针；同时对应的半单举衰变率可约束非极化胶子DiFF。采用spectator模型进行基准数值估计。

Result: Spectator模型基准计算表明不对称度约为百分量级，该量级可能已被现有的Belle数据覆盖；专门的Belle II数据集可显著改善统计精度，从而对线性极化胶子DiFF的运动学依赖施加更严格的约束。

Conclusion: χ_b0颜色单态衰变通道为线性极化胶子DiFF提供了首个直接实验探针，具备在现有及下一代B介子工厂实验（Belle/Belle II）中实现观测的可行性。

Abstract: The dihadron fragmentation function (DiFF) of a linearly polarized gluon has not yet been accessed experimentally, leaving an important aspect of spin-dependent gluon hadronization unexplored. We show that, at leading order, the color-singlet decay channel of the $P$-wave bottomonium state $χ_{b0}$ produces two energetic gluons with correlated linear polarizations. Within collinear factorization, their fragmentation into separate dihadron pairs generates an Artru--Collins-type angular correlation that provides the first direct probe of the linearly polarized gluon DiFF, while the corresponding semi-inclusive decay rate constrains the unpolarized gluon DiFF. A spectator-model benchmark indicates percent-level asymmetries, potentially within reach of existing Belle data. A dedicated Belle~II data set would substantially improve the statistical precision, enabling more stringent constraints on the kinematic dependence of the linearly polarized gluon DiFF.

</details>


### [158] [Scaling of soft QGP signatures in relativistic lead, xenon and oxygen collisions in EPOS4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10578)
*Salman Khurshid Malik,Fakhar Ul Haider,Pratibha Bhagat,Anju Bhasin,Ramni Gupta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用EPOS4事件生成器对Pb-Pb（5.02 TeV）、Xe-Xe（5.44 TeV）和O-O（5.36 TeV）碰撞进行系统研究，对比ALICE实验数据，检验小系统（O-O）中是否产生QGP液滴，并为软观测量提供统一描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中的集体膨胀和流体力学演化已得到公认，但小系统（如O-O）中是否形成费米尺度的QGP液滴仍是基本开放问题。EPOS4用于流观测量的研究尚不充分，因此需要对其集体动力学进行非平凡评估。

Method: 使用EPOS4模型对大、中、小系统（Pb-Pb、Xe-Xe、O-O）进行统一模拟，研究带电粒子多重数（dNch/dη）、π±、K±和p(p̄)的横动量谱、归一化pT关联涨落√<ΔpT,iΔpT,j>/<pT>，以及各向异性流谐波v2{2}和v3{2}随pT和中心度的变化。包含UrQMD强子级联作为后处理以正确描述重子产额。

Result: 研究发现强子级联后处理（UrQMD）对于正确描述重子产额必不可少。计算结果与ALICE实验数据（Pb-Pb和Xe-Xe）在可用范围内吻合良好。研究提供了从大系统到小系统的软观测量统一描述，并对小系统中QGP性质的信息提取给出了定量指导。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了EPOS4在流观测量的描述能力，并为从Pb-Pb到Xe-Xe再到O-O的软观测测量提供了统一的物理图景，有助于理解小系统中QGP液滴的形成及其性质。

Abstract: The collective expansion and hydrodynamic evolution in heavy-ion collisions is well-established. However, whether femtometer-scale droplets of QGP are produced in small systems at high energies remains a fundamental open question. Analysis of Pb$-$Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}$ = 5.02 TeV, Xe$-$Xe at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}$ = 5.44 TeV and O$-$O collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}$ = 5.36 TeV using EPOS4 is reported to make predictions and postdictions. The results are compared with ALICE data for Pb$-$Pb and Xe$-$Xe collisions. Charged particle multiplicity (d$N_{\mathrm{ch}}$/d$η$), transverse-momentum ($p_{\mathrm{T}}$) spectra for pions ($π^{\pm}$), kaons ($K^{\pm}$), and protons ($\text{p}(\overline{\text{p}})$) are studied. The inclusion of hadronic afterburner, UrQMD (Ultra-relativistic Quantum Molecular Dynamics) is found to be necessary to correctly describe baryon yields. $p_{\mathrm{T}}$-fluctuations are also studied via normalized $p_{\mathrm{T}}$ correlator, $\sqrt{\langle \langle Δp_{\mathrm{T},i}Δp_{\mathrm{T},j} \rangle \rangle}\mathrm{/} \langle \langle p_{\mathrm{T}} \rangle \rangle$. Lastly, anisotropic flow harmonics ($v_{\mathrm{2}} \{2\}$, $v_{\mathrm{3}} \{2\}$) are computed as a function of $p_{\mathrm{T}}$ and centrality. Since EPOS4 has not been extensively studied for flow observables, this study thereby provides a non-trivial assessment of its collective dynamics. The results are compared with experiment wherever data is available. Taken together, this study provides a unified description of soft observables from Pb$-$Pb through Xe$-$Xe down to O$-$O and offer quantitative guidance on how such collisions may inform of the properties of the QGP.

</details>


### [159] [Baryogenesis and CMB spectral distortion from Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10633)
*Zhenhao Zhang,Mingqiu Li,Sichun Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由类轴子粒子（axion-like particle）驱动的早期宇宙重子不对称性产生机制。类轴子通过改变标准模型中规范场构型的色散关系，影响Chern-Simons数，从而导致重子数破坏。文章推导了重子数与轴子背景演化的关系，并估计了该机制产生的重子不对称性，表明能够自然达到观测值。此外，还发现轴子-光子耦合会产生宇宙微波背景（CMB）光谱畸变，且该畸变在低频端趋于常数，不同于传统的y型和μ型畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙中物质与反物质的不对称性（重子不对称性）是粒子物理与宇宙学的核心问题之一。标准模型中的电弱重子数破坏机制不足以解释观测到的重子不对称性，因此需要寻找超出标准模型的新机制。本文探索利用类轴子粒子作为新来源，为理解早期宇宙重子不对称性的起源提供新的理论途径。

Method: 作者分析了类轴子粒子如何通过修改标准模型中规范场构型的色散关系，进而改变Chern-Simons数，引发重子数破坏。他们推导出重子数与轴子背景演化之间的定量关系，并估计了该机制产生的重子不对称性大小。同时，作者研究了轴子-光子耦合对CMB光谱畸变的影响，并将其与传统的y型和μ型畸变进行比较。

Result: 研究表明该机制能够自然产生与观测一致的重子不对称性。在CMB光谱畸变方面，发现轴子-光子耦合产生的畸变在低频端趋于一个常数，其频谱特征与传统的y型和μ型畸变明显不同，可作为该机制的可观测信号。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种新的重子不对称性产生机制，为早期宇宙中物质-反物质不对称性提供了可能的解释路径。同时，其独特的CMB光谱畸变特征为该机制提供了可检验的观测预言，未来精确的CMB光谱测量有望对该机制进行检验或约束。

Abstract: We discuss a mechanism for generating the baryon asymmetry in the early universe. We show that an axion-like particle can modify the related gauge field configurations in the Standard Model, thereby altering their dispersion relations. This change in the Chern-Simons number can source a violation of baryon number. We derive the relationship between the resulting baryon number and the evolution of the axion background. We estimate the baryon asymmetry produced via this mechanism and show that the observed value can be naturally achieved. We also show that axion photon coupling produces Cosmic Microwave Background spectral distortion. Our results show that the resulting distortion approaches a constant at low frequencies, unlike the conventional y-type and $μ$-type distortions.

</details>


### [160] [Evolution of the underlying event and non-extensive thermodynamics in pp collisions from RHIC to LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10717)
*Suraj Prasad,Aditya Nath Mishra,Guy Paić,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用PYTHIA8模拟研究pp碰撞中（$\sqrt{s}=62.4$ GeV至13 TeV）的粒子产生几何与热力学性质，通过分析相对领头粒子的方位角依赖性、Tsallis-Pareto参数及事件形状选择，证明底层事件（UE）主导区域可扩展到传统横向区之外，得到$40^\circ\lesssim|\Delta\varphi|\lesssim140^\circ$的扩大角度区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上底层事件的方位角区域定义受限于横向区域，需要验证该定义在从RHIC到LHC的宽能量范围内是否仍然适用；同时希望区分软硬粒子产生机制，并研究中高能pp碰撞中粒子产生的通用性和能量依赖性。

Method: 使用PYTHIA8模拟覆盖62.4 GeV至13 TeV的pp碰撞；在相对领头粒子的固定$\Delta\varphi$区间提取带电粒子横动量谱的Tsallis-Pareto参数；采用横向球度（transverse spherocity）和带电粒子平坦度（flattenicity）分类事件形状；分析带电粒子多重度与事件拓扑随方位角的依赖性。

Result: 底层事件主导区域超出传统横向区域，在RHIC到LHC能量范围内验证了$40^\circ\lesssim|\Delta\varphi|\lesssim140^\circ$的扩大角度区间；喷注状（jetty）事件表现出更大的$q$值和更低的$T_s$值；带电粒子多重度和横向球度的$\Delta\varphi$分布在能量间保持形状普适性，而Tsallis参数在近侧和远侧区域保留了领头粒子驱动的残余能量依赖性。

Conclusion: 底层事件的定义需要扩展到更宽的方位角区间，事件形状选择能够有效区分软硬过程，且某些可观测量在中等能量到LHC能量间表现出形状普适性，但热力学参数仍存在领头粒子驱动的能量依赖，这为理解pp碰撞中粒子产生的普适性和非普适性特征提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the geometrical and thermodynamical aspects of particle production in pp collisions over a broad center-of-mass energy, ranging from $\sqrt{s}=62.4$ GeV to 13 TeV using PYTHIA8. We study the azimuthal dependence of charged-particle production relative to the leading particle. This includes the study of the Tsallis--Pareto parameters extracted from the charged-particle transverse-momentum spectra in fixed $Δφ$ intervals relative to the leading charged particle. The simultaneous analysis of charged-particle multiplicity and event topology demonstrates that the underlying event (UE) dominated region can be extended beyond the conventional transverse side region across RHIC and LHC energies. To further disentangle effects from soft and hard particle production mechanisms, the study is performed in different transverse spherocity and charged-particle flattenicity classes. We demonstrate that the UE region extends beyond the conventional transverse region, validating an enlarged angular interval of $40^\circ\lesssim|Δφ|\lesssim140^\circ$ from RHIC to LHC energies. Event-shape selections reveal that jetty events consistently exhibit larger $q$ and lower $T_{\rm s}$ than isotropic events. The $Δφ$ profiles of charged-particle multiplicity and transverse spherocity remain universal in shape across collision energies despite the strong increase in overall event activity, whereas the Tsallis parameters retain a residual, leading-particle-driven energy dependence in the near- and away-side regions.

</details>


### [161] [Superfluid He-4 as Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10761)
*Zizhou Huang,Mihaela Parvu,Dmitry Budker,Ariel Zhitnitsky,Konstantin Zioutas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用超流氦-4 LHC冷却系统作为寄生式大规模暗物质探测器来检测轴子（反）夸克块（AQN）的新颖方案，并分析了AQN在金属包层中传播时产生的热效应可能对 enclosures 造成损伤的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流氦-4是探测亚GeV质量暗物质候选粒子的理想介质，已有多个合作项目搭建了相关探测器。作者希望探索一种不依赖专用探测器的新途径，利用现成的大型超流氦系统（LHC冷却装置）来检测AQN这一特殊暗物质候选体，从而以寄生方式扩大暗物质探测能力。

Method: 论文首先讨论了AQN作为暗物质候选粒子的特性，然后分析AQN在传播过程中穿过包围超流氦的金属部件时的行为。关键在于量化AQN在传播轨迹周围引起的显著加热效应，并评估该热效应对金属外壳结构完整性的影响。

Result: 研究发现AQN在金属包层中传播时会产生显著的局部加热，这些热量会损伤包围超流氦的密闭结构，这既是AQN可被探测的信号特征，也构成了工程上需要评估的结构损伤风险。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了利用既有超流氦基础设施（如LHC冷却系统）作为寄生式暗物质探测器的可行性，为AQN检测提供了一种大规模、低成本的新思路，但需进一步评估AQN对设备结构的热损伤影响。

Abstract: Superfluid helium-4 is an attractive medium for high-sensitivity detection of dark matter (DM) candidates (as well as other particles). There are several collaborations that have built and tested superfluid helium detectors, particularly focusing on the sub-GeV DM mass range. In this paper we discuss a novel idea for detecting Axion (Anti)Quark Nuggets (AQN) by using parasitically the superfluid helium-4 LHC cooling system as a unique large-scale earth bound DM detector. In the case of the AQN, we address the question of propagation in the metal parts surrounding the helium. An AQN induces significant heating around the propagation track that damages the enclosure.

</details>


### [162] [Monsters and neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10904)
*Sourendu Gupta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量纲分析和简单估算指出，质量数A>10^7且同位旋接近最大值的原子核（称为“怪物”）可能处于亚稳态，并与中子星形成连续分支。其最快衰变模式为中子蒸发，对于A>10^39的核，寿命可超过100皮秒。文章推测这类核可在双中子星并合中产生，寿命可能进一步延长，并可能被近期的引力波实验探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索原子核与中子星之间的连续过渡区域，提出一种全新的、质量数极大（A>10^7）且接近最大同位旋的亚稳态核物质形态（“怪物”核），并探讨其天文物理可观测性。

Method: 采用量纲分析和简单物理估算的方法，分析此类大质量、接近最大同位旋的原子核的稳定性条件，估计其最快衰变模式（中子蒸发）的寿命，并推测其在中子星并合事件中的形成机制与可探测信号。

Result: 发现A>10^7且同位旋接近最大值的原子核可能处于亚稳态，该分支与中子星连续衔接。中子蒸发是主要衰变通道，A>10^39时寿命可超过100皮秒。在双中子星并合中这些核可能形成且寿命更长，其存在有望被近未来引力波实验探测。

Conclusion: “怪物”核代表了一种介于原子核与中子星之间的新型亚稳态物质状态，其理论预测为引力波天文学提供了新的潜在观测目标，值得进一步深入研究其形成机制与探测途径。

Abstract: Using dimensional arguments and other simple estimates, it is pointed out that a branch of nuclei with mass number A>10^7 could be metastable if they have close to maximal isospin. This branch is continuous with neutron stars. The fastest decay mode of these hypothetical metastable nuclei (which we call monsters) is through neutron evaporation. We present an estimate of the lifetime and show it could be more than 100 ps for A>10^{39}. We speculate about their creation in binary neutron star mergers, where we argue that their lifetimes could be extended further, and their presence could be detectable in gravity wave experiments of the near future.

</details>


### [163] [Gluonic nucleon energy correlators and fracture functions for Color Glass Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10955)
*Heikki Mäntysaari,Yu Shi,Yossathorn Tawabutr,Xuan-Bo Tong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用色玻璃凝聚（CGC）有效理论在eikonal精度下研究了核子能量关联函数（NECs）与断裂函数，发现非极化靶中只有非极化和线性极化胶子分量非零，且由伴随偶极S矩阵决定。线性极化胶子NEC h1^{t,g}在靶碎裂区产生特征性的cos2φ方位角不对称性，该不对称性对饱和尺度特别敏感，数值分析显示其具有显著的核抑制效应，为未来电子-离子对撞机探测胶子饱和提供了新窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深非弹性散射（DIS）的靶碎裂区（TFR）通过核子能量关联函数和断裂函数探测小x下的核子与原子核结构，为未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上研究胶子饱和效应提供新的观测量。

Method: 采用色玻璃凝聚（CGC）有效理论在eikonal精度下计算胶子NECs和断裂函数，分析非极化靶中的各个胶子分量，并通过数值计算研究DIS能量模式中cos2φ方位角不对称性及其核依赖行为。

Result: 发现非极化靶中仅非极化和线性极化胶子分量非零，均由伴随偶极S矩阵决定；线性极化胶子NEC h1^{t,g}产生cos2φ方位角不对称性，该不对称性由线性极化胶子NEC与极化夸克NEC的比值控制，对饱和尺度敏感；数值结果显示该不对称性具有显著的核抑制效应。

Conclusion: 靶碎裂区的能量模式中的方位角不对称性为揭示胶子饱和起始提供了一个新颖而灵敏的探针，有望在未来的电子-离子对撞机上得到验证。

Abstract: Nucleon energy correlators (NECs) and fracture functions provide novel tools for probing nucleon and nuclear structure at small $x$ through measurements in the target fragmentation region (TFR) of deep inelastic scattering (DIS). We investigate gluonic NECs and fracture functions using the Color Glass Condensate effective theory at eikonal accuracy. We find that only the unpolarized and linearly polarized gluon components in an unpolarized target are nonvanishing at this order, and that both are determined by the adjoint dipole $S$-matrix. Furthermore, we show that the linearly polarized gluonic NEC $h_{1}^{t,g}$ generates a characteristic $\cos 2φ$ azimuthal asymmetry in the DIS energy pattern in the TFR. Unlike analogous observables in the current fragmentation region, this asymmetry is governed by the ratio of the linearly polarized gluon NEC $h_{1}^{t,g}$ to the unpolarized quark NEC $f_{1}^{q}$, making it particularly sensitive to the saturation scale. Our numerical analysis shows that this asymmetry exhibits substantial nuclear suppression, providing a novel window into the onset of gluon saturation at the future Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [164] [TauPolaris: reconstructing tau lepton polarimetric vectors with conditional normalizing flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10961)
*Daniel Winterbottom,Lucas Russell*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了TauPolaris，一个利用条件归一化流重建tau轻子极化矢量的工具。通过估计不可探测的中微子动量，该方法能够重建tau自旋观测量的完整条件密度，而非仅做点回归。在HL-LHC模拟中，该方法在量子纠缠探测（≥4.3σ）、CP破坏灵敏度（提升18%）以及Z→ττ背景抑制等应用中均优于MSE损失的神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tau轻子衰变产物的运动学内含其自旋信息，可用于研究产生它的过程的自旋关联和CP结构。最优的自旋观测量是极化矢量，但它需要tau衰变中产生的中微子动量，而这些中微子无法被探测，必须通过推断获得。现有方法采用点回归（point regression）来预测中微子动量，无法提供不确定性估计，且重建的自旋观测量分辨率有限。

Method: 使用条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）建模中微子运动学的完整条件密度，而非仅做点估计。该方法对每个事件同时提供最可能的中微子运动学配置及其不确定性。利用得到的极化矢量重建tau自旋观测量，并在三个应用中验证：H→ττ衰变中量子纠缠的探测、CP破坏灵敏度测量、以及Z→ττ背景抑制。

Result: 在包含探测器分辨率效应的模拟LHC质子-质子碰撞中：1）在HL-LHC下，H→ττ产生的tau轻子的量子纠缠存在与否的区分显著度至少为4.3σ；2）对H→ττ衰变中CP破坏的灵敏度较MSE损失训练的网络提升18%；3）提出了抑制Z→ττ背景的新观测量。重建的自旋观测量分辨率优于使用均方误差损失训练的网络。

Conclusion: TauPolaris通过条件归一化流建模中微子运动学的完整分布，为tau极化矢量重建提供了比传统点回归方法更优的方案。该方法不仅提供点估计还给出不确定性估计，在多个物理应用中展示了对量子纠缠探测、CP破坏测量和背景抑制方面的显著改进，是一项适用于LHC及HL-LHC物理分析的工具。

Abstract: The kinematics of tau lepton decay products depend on the tau spin, giving access to the spin correlations and CP structure of the process that produced them. The optimal spin observable is the polarimetric vector, which points along the most likely direction of the tau spin. Determining it requires the momenta of the neutrinos produced in the tau decays, which escape detection and must therefore be inferred. We present TauPolaris, a tool for reconstructing tau polarimetric vectors by estimating the undetected neutrino momenta with a conditional normalizing flow. Rather than performing a point regression, the method models the full conditional density of the neutrino kinematics, providing both the most likely configuration for each event and an estimate of its uncertainty. The resolution of the reconstructed spin observables improves on that obtained from networks trained with a mean-squared-error loss. Using simulated LHC proton-proton collisions, including detector resolution effects, we demonstrate the method in three applications. For tau leptons produced in Higgs boson decays, we show that the presence of quantum entanglement can be distinguished from its absence with a significance of at least $4.3σ$ at the High-Luminosity LHC. We demonstrate an 18% improvement in the sensitivity to CP violation in $H\rightarrowττ$ decays. Finally, we introduce new variables for suppressing the $Z\rightarrowττ$ background in Higgs boson searches.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-Channel Di-Higgs Production in the scNMSSM: From Exotic 8b to Boosted 8tau Signatures at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11040)
*Marwa Telba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了半约束NMSSM（scNMSSM）模型在大λ/低tanβ参数下，HL-LHC（√s=13.6 TeV，L=3000 fb⁻¹）多通道双希格斯信号的前景。从66个SM-like候选点中选取4个基准点，涵盖三种双希格斯拓扑（8b、增强8τ、SM-like）。通过完整的模拟链（MadGraph5_aMC@NLO + Pythia 8.316 + Delphes 3）对每个基准点模拟10⁵个信号事件。结果表明：8b拓扑可实现约14,000-16,000个信号事件，有望达到5σ灵敏度；增强8τ拓扑中82.4%的τ对ΔR<0.4，需要专用的增强a1标记器；BP4作为SM-like参考点。Higgs自耦合修正因子κλ普遍抑制在[0.884, 0.951]区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC有望发现双希格斯（HH）产生，但其产生截面极小，需要寻找超越标准模型的增强通道。scNMSSM在大λ/低tanβ区域提供丰富的双希格斯拓扑（包括8b和增强8τ等奇异末态），但这些拓扑尚未被现有ATLAS和CMS搜索覆盖。作者旨在系统评估这些通道在HL-LHC上的信号前景，为未来实验搜索策略提供方向。

Method: 从66个SM-like候选点中选取4个基准点（BP1/BP3对应8b拓扑，BP2对应增强8τ拓扑，BP4为SM-like参考），使用完整信号模拟链（MadGraph5_aMC@NLO + Pythia 8.316 + Delphes 3）对每个基准点生成10⁵个信号事件。背景估计基于已发表的LHC分析数据，采用简化的背景模型进行灵敏度投影。对b-jet效率和τ对ΔR分布进行了详细分析。

Result: 8b拓扑（BP1: m_{a1}=47.4 GeV, BP3: m_{a1}=51.9 GeV）的ε(≥4b)~14%给出S~14,000-16,000事件，在简化背景模型下与5σ灵敏度兼容。增强8τ拓扑（BP2: m_{a1}=8.2 GeV）中，γ~7.5的洛伦兹增强使82.4%的同a1 τ对满足ΔR<0.4，ε(≥2τ)=38.4%（为SM基线的21倍），但需专用增强a1标记器。BP4与现有HL-LHC HH投影一致。κλ在[0.884, 0.951]范围内普遍低于1。

Conclusion: scNMSSM在大λ/低tanβ区域为HL-LHC提供了丰富且可探的双希格斯信号通道。8b拓扑有潜力达到5σ发现灵敏度；增强8τ拓扑是现有ATLAS/CMS搜索完全未覆盖的新通道，强烈建议开发专门的子喷注分析策略。SM-like参考点与标准HL-HH投影一致。整体上，该研究为HL-LHC的di-Higgs搜索扩展提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We investigate multi-channel di-Higgs signal prospects in the semi-constrained Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (scNMSSM) at large-lambda/low-tan(beta) at the HL-LHC with sqrt(s) = 13.6 TeV and L = 3000 fb^{-1}. Building on our previous work, four benchmark points are selected from 66 viable SM-like candidates, spanning three di-Higgs topologies: exotic multi-b (h1h1 -> 4a1 -> 8b, BP1/BP3), boosted multi-tau (h1h1 -> 4a1 -> 8tau, BP2), and SM-like (BP4). A complete signal simulation chain (MadGraph5_aMC@NLO + Pythia 8.316 + Delphes 3) is employed for 10^5 signal events per benchmark point. Background yields are estimated from published LHC analyses and are used only for indicative sensitivity projections. For the 8b topology (BP1: m_{a1} = 47.4 GeV, BP3: m_{a1} = 51.9 GeV), b-jet efficiencies of epsilon(>=4b) ~ 14% yield S ~ 14,000-16,000 events, potentially compatible with 5-sigma sensitivity under the simplified background model adopted. For the boosted 8tau topology (BP2: m_{a1} = 8.2 GeV), 82.4% of same-a1 tau-pairs have Delta R(tau,tau) < 0.4, below the standard jet cone radius, due to the large Lorentz boost gamma ~ 7.5. This topology yields epsilon(>=2tau) = 38.4%, a factor of ~21 above the SM baseline, but requires dedicated boosted a1 taggers due to irreducible multi-tau backgrounds. BP4 serves as a SM-like reference, consistent with existing HL-LHC HH projections. The Higgs self-coupling modifier kappa_lambda in [0.884, 0.951] is universally suppressed below unity. The boosted a1 topology of BP2 is not covered by any existing ATLAS or CMS di-Higgs search, strongly motivating dedicated sub-jet analysis strategies at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [166] [Tricritical dynamics in $\mathrm{^3He}$-$\mathrm{^4He}$ mixtures and QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11065)
*Maneesha Pradeep,Johannes V. Roth,Lorenz von Smekal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用实时泛函重整化群研究$^3$He-$^4$He混合物和双无质量夸克味QCD中超临界点附近的普适临界动力学，揭示了三临界区域如何将两味手征相变的$O(4)$ Model G动力学与QCD临界点的Model H动力学联系起来，并展示了强动态标度与次扩散能量密度涨落的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三临界点附近的普适临界动力学是连接不同临界行为的重要理论问题：一方面$^3$He-$^4$He混合物的λ线和三临界点涉及平面$O(2)$对称性，另一方面QCD双味手征相变（Model G动力学）和显式破缺手征对称性下的QCD临界点（Model H动力学）分别具有不同的普适动力学类别。揭示这些不同临界动力学之间的过渡机制是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用实时泛函重整化群（real-time FRG）形式，系统研究三临界区域中临界动力学的重整化群流。通过引入额外的次扩散（subdiffusive）能量类密度，分析三临界动力学如何继承Model G的强动态标度（即序参量和守恒$O(N)$荷的临界涨落以相同速率弛豫），并在线性混合框架下处理显式对称性破缺对自由度的重整化作用。

Result: 论文表明：在存在额外次扩散能量类密度时，三临界动力学继承了Model G的强动态标度；平面$O(2)$情形（与$^3$He-$^4$He混合物的λ线和三临界点相关）是强标度的一个重要极限情形；在QCD中包含有限轻夸克质量的显式对称性破缺后，三临界动力学的能量类密度与$Z_2$序参量线性混合，最终产生QCD临界点附近每重子熵的特征性慢涨落。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的理论框架，将三临界点、$O(4)$ Model G手征相变动力学和Model H QCD临界点动力学联系起来，揭示了能量类密度与序参量混合机制在连接不同普适类中的关键作用，为$^3$He-$^4$He混合物和QCD临界点的动力学普适性提供了统一图景。

Abstract: In this paper we study the universal critical dynamics near the tricritical points in $\mathrm{^3He}$-$\mathrm{^4He}$ mixtures and QCD with two massless quark flavors. In particular, we use the real-time formulation of the functional renormalization group to understand how the tricritical region connects the $O(4)$ Model G dynamics of the two-flavor chiral phase transition to that of Model H for the QCD critical point with explicitly broken chiral symmetry. We show that in presence of an additional subdiffusive energy-like density, the tricritical dynamics inherits the strong dynamic scaling of Model G, where the critical fluctuations of order parameter and conserved $O(N)$ charges relax at identical rates. The strong scaling in the planar $O(2)$ case relevant for the $λ$-line and the tricritical point in the liquid $\mathrm{^3He}$-$\mathrm{^4He}$ mixtures arises as an interesting limiting case. Including the explicit symmetry breaking by the finite light-quark masses in QCD, the energy-like density of the tricritical dynamics mixes linearly with the $Z_2$ order parameter to eventually produce the characteristic slow fluctuations of the entropy per baryon near the QCD critical point.

</details>


### [167] [Hilbert Series of Pseudoscalar Mesons: Operators and Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11118)
*Joydeep Chakrabortty,Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Siddhartha Karmakar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用希尔伯特级数方法，在SU(3)_L × SU(3)_R × SU(2)_{L_H} × SU(2)_{R_H}全局味对称性及位移对称性下，构建了由赝标量介子构成的完整非冗余算符基，并通过将CKM矩阵元提升为spurion场来系统纳入SM弱相互作用，从而提供了重介子弱衰变振幅的模型无关参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为重介子两体和三体弱衰变提供一个模型无关的完整算符基，以便系统化地理解可能的味关系（amplitude sum rules），并建立有效算符与其紫外起源之间的关联。

Method: 采用希尔伯特级数（Hilbert series）方法，在全局限对称性及相应的位移对称性约束下，枚举并构建由赝标量介子场构成的非冗余算符基。通过将CKM矩阵元提升为在全局对称性下变换的spurion场，将SM弱相互作用纳入框架。利用所构建的算符基推导振幅求和规则，并提出一种"unfolding"程序以连接有效算符与其可能的紫外来源。

Result: 所得算符基以Wilson系数作为约化强子振幅，提供了重介子弱衰变的模型无关参数化。框架成功复现了已知的味关系，并基于对称性约束的算符基预测了额外的恒等式（新振幅求和规则）。所有振幅求和规则均直接从算符基的结构中自然导出。

Conclusion: 该对称性驱动的算符基方法为重介子弱衰变提供了一个统一且系统的描述框架，不仅重现了文献中已知的味关系，还发现了新的对称性约束恒等式，并为连接低能有效场论与紫外物理提供了途径。

Abstract: We construct the complete non-redundant basis of operators built from pseudoscalar mesons using the Hilbert series method, subject to the global flavor symmetry $SU(3)_L \times SU(3)_R \times SU(2)_{L_H} \times SU(2)_{R_H}$, together with the underlying shift symmetry. The SM weak interactions are incorporated systematically by promoting the CKM matrix elements to spurion fields transforming under the global symmetry. The resulting operator basis provides a model-independent parametrization of two- and three-body weak decay amplitudes of heavy mesons, with the corresponding Wilson coefficients serving as reduced hadronic amplitudes. We demonstrate that all amplitude sum rules follow directly from the structure of the operator basis and propose an unfolding procedure that connects the effective operators to their possible ultraviolet origins. Our framework reproduces the known flavor relations while predicting additional identities arising from the symmetry-constrained operator basis.

</details>


### [168] [QCD Vacuum in an Inhomogeneous Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11144)
*Prabal Adhikari,Brian C. Tiburzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用手征微扰论研究非均匀磁场对QCD真空的影响，在特定可精确求解的磁场轮廓下，通过维数正规化处理重正则化问题，计算了零温自由能、手征凝聚和诱导真空流等可观测量的空间分辨行为，并与局域常数近似进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD真空在磁场下的响应是强相互作用领域的重要问题，但以往研究多假设均匀磁场。实际物理场景（如重离子碰撞、致密星体）中的磁场往往具有空间非均匀性，因此需要在手征微扰论框架下系统地研究非均匀磁场对真空结构的影响，揭示其空间响应与非局域结构。

Method: 采用手征微扰论框架；选取沿一个空间方向局域化的特定磁场轮廓，使得对应的量子力学问题可精确求解；利用维数正规化处理正规化与重正化；在chiral展开中分析磁场梯度引入的新算符效应（出现在次次领头阶）；在次领头阶计算自由能与手征凝聚的积分量和空间分辨局域量，并推导非均匀磁场诱导的真空流。

Result: 非均匀磁场的梯度在手征微扰论中需要额外算符，但其贡献出现在次次领头阶（NNLO），因此在次领头阶（NLO）即可无需引入未定参数地确定真空可观测量对磁场的依赖；成功计算了零温自由能与手征凝聚的积分量和空间分辨量，并与局域常数近似对比，揭示空间响应与非局域结构；还推导了磁场非均匀性诱导的真空电流。

Conclusion: 手征微扰论可以在NLO阶无参数地描述非均匀磁场下QCD真空的可观测量，非均匀性效应仅在更高阶显现；通过与局域常数近似比较可直接探测磁化QCD真空的空间响应和非局域特性，为理解非均匀电磁环境下强相互作用的真空性质提供了定量工具。

Abstract: The effect of an inhomogeneous magnetic field on the QCD vacuum is addressed using the framework of chiral perturbation theory. The magnetic field is chosen to be localized along one spatial direction, with a profile for which the underlying quantum mechanical problem is exactly solvable. Particular attention is paid to regularization and renormalization using dimensional regularization. While the non-vanishing gradient of the magnetic field requires additional operators in chiral perturbation theory, their effect occurs at next-to-next-to-leading order in the chiral expansion. Consequently, the magnetic field dependence of equilibrium vacuum observables can be determined at next-to-leading order without undetermined parameters. We compute the zero-temperature free energy and chiral condensate for the inhomogeneous background, both as integrated quantities as well as spatially resolved local observables. Comparison with locally constant approximations enables a direct probe of the spatial response and nonlocal structure of the magnetized QCD vacuum. We additionally derive the induced vacuum current associated with the inhomogeneity of the magnetic field.

</details>


### [169] [$|ΔI|=3/2$ non-leptonic hyperon decays in covariant baryon chiral perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11176)
*Jie Xu,Jun-Xu Lu,Rui-Xiang Shi,Li-Sheng Geng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变重子手征微扰论框架下，使用扩展离壳重整化方案，重新审视了非轻子超子衰变中的|ΔI|=3/2振幅。研究发现相对论修正单独作用效果温和，而明确引入十重态重子（特别是在一致耦合方案下）以及π圈贡献能显著改善拟合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期BESIII实验对非轻子超子衰变测量的启发，作者希望重新评估这些衰变中|ΔI|=3/2振幅的理论描述。早期在重重子手征微扰论中的分析使用了受限的图拓扑集合，本文旨在考察在该框架下引入协变（相对论）处理、明确十重态重子自由度以及不同耦合方案后，理论拟合质量能否得到改善。

Method: 作者在协变重子手征微扰论（covariant baryon chiral perturbation theory）框架下，采用扩展离壳（extended-on-mass-shell, EOMS）重整化方案。沿用早期重手征微扰论分析中相同的受限图拓扑集合，系统考察了以下因素的影响：（1）相对论修正；（2）明确引入自旋-3/2十重态重子；（3）不同的自旋-3/2耦合方案（包括一致耦合方案）；（4）π圈（pion-loop）贡献。

Result: 结果显示：相对论效应单独导致的变化比较温和；十重态贡献（尤其在一至耦合方案下）显著提高了拟合质量；π圈贡献进一步降低了χ²值。一致耦合方案在描述所选|ΔI|=3/2振幅方面被证明是至关重要的。

Conclusion: 作者强调，在十重态扇区采用一致耦合方案对于准确描述选定的|ΔI|=3/2非轻子超子衰变振幅具有关键意义，这为理解超子衰变中的手征动力学提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: Inspired by the recent BESIII measurements of non-leptonic hyperon decays, we reexamine their $|ΔI|=3/2$ amplitudes in covariant baryon chiral perturbation theory with the extended-on-mass-shell renormalization scheme. Using the same restricted set of diagrammatic topologies as in the early analyses in heavy baryon chiral perturbation theory, we assess the effects of relativistic corrections, explicit decuplet baryons, different spin-$3/2$ coupling schemes, and pion-loop contributions. Our results show that relativistic effects alone lead to only mild changes, while decuplet contributions, especially in the consistent-coupling scheme, significantly improve the fit quality. Pion-loop contributions further reduce the $χ^2$ values. We highlight the importance of the consistent coupling scheme in the decuplet sector for describing the selected $|ΔI|=3/2$ amplitudes.

</details>

### [170] [Geometrize the nongeometric space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09958)
*Tran Chi Quy,Sonnet Nguyen Quang Hung*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在下降理论框架下，提出了一种严格且系统的数学形式化方法来描述非几何R-空间，将其视为由底流形各局部坐标域上拟三角拟Hopf代数的局部数据粘合而成的双范畴值层，从而为R-空间提供了全局几何定义，同时保留了其局部非几何的内在特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论R-空间同时具有非交换和非结合结构，且已知局部是非几何的。然而以往研究主要通过局部弦位置坐标的代数关系来描述R-空间，缺乏精确的全局几何定义。因此需要一种严格的数学框架来给出R-空间的全局几何定义。

Method: 作者在下降理论框架下提出形式化方法：将非几何R-空间视为底流形每个局部坐标域上仅携带拟三角拟Hopf代数的局部数据，然后通过一致地粘合这些局部数据构造全局几何对象。研究表明所得到的全局结构既不是普通空间也不是全局定义的代数，而是一个双范畴值层（bicategory-valued stack）。

Result: 证明了R-空间的适当全局结构是一个双范畴值层，这一结构给出了R-空间的全局几何定义，同时保留了其局部非几何的内在特性。

Conclusion: R-空间不应被理解为普通空间或全局代数，而应被理解为双范畴值层的全局几何对象。这一框架既提供了严格的全局定义，又保持了R-空间局部非几何的本质特征。

Abstract: Noncommutative and nonassociative geometry has attracted much attention in both mathematics and physics communities. A prominent example is a stringy $R$-space over an underlying manifold, which exhibits both noncommutative and nonassociative structures and is known to be locally nongeometric. However, previous studies have mainly described $R$-space through algebraic relations of local string position coordinates, instead of a precise global geometric definition. In this paper, we propose a rigorous and systematic mathematical formulation of nongeometric $R$-space within the framework of descent theory. The central idea is to regard nongeometric $R$-space as carrying only local data of a quasitriangular quasi-Hopf algebra on each local coordinate domain of the underlying manifold, which are then glued coherently together to produce a global geometric object. We show that the appropriate global structure is not an ordinary space or a globally defined algebra, but a bicategory-valued stack. This provides a global and geometric definition of $R$-space while still preserving its locally nongeometric intrinsic character.

</details>


### [171] [3-dimensional TQFTs from derived categories of quantum group representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10285)
*Cris Negron*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明任意有限模张量范畴A的导出∞-范畴D(A)支持一个三维拓扑量子场论（TQFT），该理论以对称幺半函子形式从带标记曲面和合适装饰的边缘流形映射到dg向量空间的∞-范畴。通过同伦截断可去除标记，得到二维射影映射类群作用和高维（三维）幂级数值不变量，并作为N=4超对称QFT拓扑A-twist的数学形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有限模张量范畴相关拓扑量子场论建立严格的数学基础，将物理中的N=4超对称量子场论拓扑扭转形式化，并连接已有的映射类群作用与Hochschild上同调等代数不变量。

Method: 利用导出∞-范畴D(A)构造对称幺半函子TQFT，定义从带D(A)对象标记的曲面及装饰边缘流形到dg向量空间∞-范畴的函子。通过同伦截断消除标记，分析状态空间与线性化映射空间的自然同构，并在三维情形获得幂级数不变量。进一步讨论沿局部系统变形及与Langlands对偶群相关的量子群表示变形。

Result: 建立了三维TQFT形式体系，状态空间与D(A)的线性化映射空间自然等同；同伦截断后消除标记得到无标记TQFT，产生二维射影映射类群作用（包括Hochschild上同调上的射影SL_2(Z)作用），以及三维幂级数值的纽结不变量、模张量范畴不变量和闭三维流形不变量。

Conclusion: 该导出场论为拓扑A-twist的N=4超对称TQFT提供数学形式化，其映射类群作用预期与Lentner等和Schweigert-Woike的结果一致，并可能沿Langlands对偶群通过局部系统的形变推广。

Abstract: For any finite modular tensor category A, we show that the associated derived $\infty$-category D(A) supports a topological quantum field theory in dimension 3. This TQFT takes the form of a symmetric monoidal functor from an $\infty$-category of surfaces with markings by objects in D(A), and appropriately decorated bordisms, to the $\infty$-category of dg vector spaces. We show that the state spaces in this theory are naturally identified with linearized mapping spaces for D(A).
  Though our TQFT requires markings from the derived $\infty$-category, we show that all markings can be removed after taking a homotopy truncation. The resulting unmarked TQFT produces projective mapping class group actions on cohomology in dimension 2, and in particular a projective SL_2(Z)-action on Hochschild cohomology. We expect these mapping class group actions to recover those of Lentner et al. arxiv:2003.06527 and Schweigert-Woike arxiv:2004.14343. In dimension 3 we obtain power-series valued knot invariants, and power-series valued invariants for modular tensor categories. We also obtain power-series invariants for closed 3-manifold, though these can already be calculated at the abelian level.
  Our derived field theories are proposed as mathematical formalizations for topological A-twists of certain N=4 supersymmetric QFTs, in dimension 3. Following physical principles, we discuss the possibility of deforming our TQFTs along local systems via an analogous (conjectural) deformation of quantum group representations along the Langlands dual group.

</details>


### [172] [Hochschild Cohomology, Modular Tensor Categories, and Mapping Class Groups II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11193)
*Simon Lentner,Svea Nora Mierach,Christoph Schweigert,Yorck Sommerhaeuser*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在正特征域上，针对有限群Drinfel'd double对应的非半单模范畴，显式计算了曲面映射类群在派生共形块空间上的作用，并证明了该表示与普通共形块空间上的表示一般不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作中作者已将模范畴（不必半单）上的映射类群作用推广到派生共形块空间，但尚未给出具体实例的显式计算。选择正特征下有限群Drinfel'd double作为检验案例，旨在验证这一推广是否确实产生新的表示。

Method: 将Lyubashenko关于映射类群表示的方法与表示簇（representation varieties）理论建立联系，利用这一联系对Drinfel'd double（有限群、正特征域）情形进行显式计算。

Result: 通过显式计算发现，在派生共形块空间上的映射类群表示与普通共形块空间上的表示存在本质差异，即一般情况下二者并不相同。

Conclusion: 派生共形块空间上的映射类群表示构成了一类新的、不同于经典表示的对象，这为理解非半单模范畴及其几何应用提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In the first part of this work, we have, for a not necessarily semisimple modular category, generalized the action of the mapping class groups of surfaces on the spaces of conformal blocks to the so-called derived block spaces. In the second part presented here, we compute this action explicitly in the case of Drinfel'd doubles of finite groups over fields of positive characteristic. To do that, we connect Lyubashenko's approach to mapping class group representations with the theory of representation varieties. In this way, we are able to show that the mapping class group representations on the derived block spaces are in general different from those on the ordinary block spaces.

</details>
